### [1] [An Overview of Einstein 4-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03033)
*Claude LeBrun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 这是一篇基于作者在巴黎数学科学基金会2026年卓越教席系列讲座（主题为“爱因斯坦度量、四流形与微分拓扑”）的综述性文章。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文的动机是将讲座系列中关于爱因斯坦度量、四维流形与微分拓扑交叉领域的内容以综述形式系统化呈现，使更广泛的读者能够了解该领域的前沿进展。

Method: 作为一篇综述性（expository）文章，其方法并非提出新的技术贡献，而是对已有研究成果进行系统梳理、整合与阐释，涵盖爱因斯坦度量、四维流形的几何与分析以及微分拓扑之间的联系。

Result: 摘要本身未给出具体结果，仅交代了文章的来源背景（作者2026年在巴黎的系列讲座）与综述性质。

Conclusion: 该文章旨在以综述形式向数学界传播爱因斯坦度量、四流形与微分拓扑领域的核心思想与最新进展。

Abstract: This expository article is based in part on the author's lecture series, "Einstein Metrics, Four-Manifolds, and Differential Topology," which was delivered as a 2026 Cours de la Chaire d'Excellence for the Fondation Sciences Mathématiques de Paris.

</details>


### [2] [Maslov Indicies In Symplectic Geometry Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03061)
*Sylvain E. Cappell,Edward Y. Miller*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了三十年前与Ronnie Lee教授合作的Maslov指标公理化工作，提出了两种新的Maslov指标定义方法，并与Her和Zhong近期基于Lidskii、Gelfand、Salomon和Zehnder早期工作所定义的一个辛矩阵路径不变量进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maslov指标是Lagrangian对连续、分段光滑路径的一个整数不变量，在辛几何和动力系统中有重要应用。作者希望通过提供新的定义方法来深化对Maslov指标的理解，并将其与近期Her和Zhong定义的辛矩阵路径不变量进行比较分析。

Method: 作者在原有公理化体系基础上，新增两种Maslov指标的定义方法，并系统比较这些新定义与Her和Zhong近期提出的辛矩阵路径不变量的异同，后者受Lidskii、Gelfand、Salomon和Zehnder早期工作的启发。

Result: 论文提供了两种新的Maslov指标定义方法，并阐明了这些新方法与Her-Zhong不变量（以及其理论根源Lidskii-Gelfand-Salomon-Zehnder工作）之间的关系与区别。

Conclusion: 通过新增定义方法并与相关近期工作进行对比，本文进一步丰富了Maslov指标理论，为后续研究提供了更全面的视角和工具。

Abstract: Thirty years ago in ``On the Maslov Index'' the present authors with their late collaborator Prof. Ronnie Lee of Yale University presented an axiomatization of the Maslov index, an integer-valued invariant of continuous, piecewise smooth paths of pairs of Lagrangians. Four distinct methods of definition of such Maslov indicies were given; each satisfying the axioms. Here two more are offered and comparisons are made to the recent work of Her and Zhong defining an invariant of a path of symplectic matrices motivated by earlier work of Lidskii and Gelfand, Salomon and Zehnder.

</details>


### [3] [Uniqueness of tangent flows in free boundary flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03185)
*Sourav Ghosh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了带自由边界的平均曲率流在半柱状自收缩子奇点处的切线流唯一性，具体而言，当切线流形如 S^{n-k} × R^k_+ 或 S^{n-k}_+ × R^k 时，其切线流是唯一的，推广了经典柱状奇点唯一性理论到自由边界情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均曲率流奇点分析中，切线流的唯一性是理解奇点结构的关键问题。经典结果已证明了柱状奇点（即无自由边界情形）的切线流唯一性，但带自由边界的平均曲率流（如嵌入在某个支撑流形中的超曲面）的奇点唯一性尚属空白，本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 作者针对两类半柱状自收缩子（半无限圆柱 S^{n-k} × R^k_+ 与圆柱的半空间截面 S^{n-k}_+ × R^k）建立切线流唯一性。具体技术手段涉及对自由边界平均曲率流的单调性公式分析、在奇点处与半柱状自收缩子的比较论证，以及利用刚性或间隙性质排除非唯一性。

Result: 成功证明了在上述两类半柱状自收缩子形式的切线流意义下，带自由边界的平均曲率流在奇点处的切线流是唯一的，给出了自由边界平均曲率流奇点分类问题中柱状奇点唯一性定理的完整类比。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了自由边界平均曲率流中柱状奇点的切线流唯一性，为后续研究自由边界MCF奇点结构的精细分析（如奇点的正则化与解的延拓）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove uniqueness of tangent flows for mean curvature flow with free boundary at singularities modeled on half-cylindrical self-shrinkers. More precisely, if a tangent flow is of the form $\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$ or $\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k $, then it is unique. This provides the free-boundary analogue of the uniqueness theory for cylindrical singularities.

</details>

Abstract: Let $(M,g,J)$ be a closed Kähler manifold satisfying $\operatorname{Ric}\geqslant g$. We establish improved Liouville theorems for the Euler--Lagrange equations associated with the Beckner--Sobolev inequalities by incorporating the first positive eigenvalue of the $\bar\partial$-Laplacian into a differential-identity argument. As a consequence, we obtain improved Sobolev and Beckner inequalities that refine the known Riemannian and Kähler estimates when the first eigenvalue is sufficiently large. We also derive new upper bounds for the diameter of $(M,g)$.

### [5] [Nonuniqueness of Model Potentials and Weak Geodesic Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03296)
*Xia Xiao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在复射影直线ℂP¹上建立了两个非唯一性现象：其一，不同的归一化正质量模型势可以具有相同的非多次极Monge–Ampère测度，且零接触集处处一致、每点Lelong数为零、各级乘子理想层相同；其二，构造了不同的极值测试曲线，其零接触集逐点一致，但对应的反向Legendre变换是不同但有界且从零出发的弱测地射线，且具有相同的逐点右初始切线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Monge–Ampère理论中解的唯一性问题，具体考察在低维复流形(ℂP¹)上，Monge–Ampère测度、零接触集、Lelong数、乘子理想层等对象能否唯一决定所对应的势函数或测地射线。这关乎正质量模型势的分类问题和弱测地射线延拓的唯一性边界。

Method: 利用接触集公式(contact-set formula)作为核心工具，在ℂP¹上系统构造具体的归一化正质量模型势反例，并通过逆Legendre变换建立测试曲线与弱测地射线之间的对偶关系，比较两者的点态初始切线和零接触集性质。

Result: 证明了两组非唯一性结论：(1) 在ℂP¹上存在不同的归一化正质量模型势，其非多次极Monge–Ampère测度相同，零接触集在Fubini–Study度量下几乎处处一致，且两者每点的Lelong数均为零、任意正尺度下的乘子理想层均相同；(2) 构造了不同的极值测试曲线，其零接触集在每个参数点处逐点一致，但对应的逆Legendre变换是不同的有界弱测地射线（从零出发），且具有相同的逐点右初始切线。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了Monge–Ampère理论中多个经典不变量的不敏感性问题：即使测度、接触集、Lelong数和乘子理想层均一致，对应的势函数仍可不同；同时，测试曲线与弱测地射线之间的Legendre对偶在初始切线信息上也存在信息损失。这对理解Kähler几何中规范势的分类和测地射线延拓的唯一性具有重要启示。

Abstract: We establish two nonuniqueness phenomena on $\mathbb{CP}^1$. First, distinct normalized positive-mass model potentials can have the same non-pluripolar Monge--Ampère measure. By the contact-set formula, their zero-contact sets agree $ω_{\mathrm{FS}}$-almost everywhere. Moreover, both have zero Lelong number at every point and identical multiplier ideal sheaves at every positive scale. Second, we construct distinct maximal test curves whose zero-contact sets agree pointwise at every parameter. Their inverse Legendre transforms are distinct bounded weak geodesic rays from zero with the same pointwise right initial tangent.

</details>


### [6] [Singularity Models of Finite-Time Kähler-Ricci Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03332)
*Frederick Tsz-Ho Fong,Hung Tran*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究由圆周丛在Kähler-Einstein流形乘积上的1-参数叶状结构构造的紧流形上Kähler-Ricci流的奇点类型与模型。在先前工作处理"two-bolt"情形后，本文继续研究更微妙的"nut-bolt"和"two-nut"情形，证明所有三种情形下奇点必为Type I型，并进一步刻画了缩放序列的pointed Cheeger-Gromov极限模型，证明极限必为(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) × (C^k, flat)的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过DW2011和WW等文献中考虑的ansatz，在由圆周丛构造的紧流形上研究Kähler-Ricci流在奇点处的行为。作者此前已处理"two-bolt"情形，本文旨在推进对更微妙、更复杂的"nut-bolt"（一端nut塌缩、一端bolt）和"two-nut"（两端均为nut塌缩）情形的奇点类型与极限模型的完整刻画。

Method: 利用circle-bundle ansatz构造度量并研究Kähler-Ricci流。对三种端点闭合情形（two-bolt、nut-bolt、two-nut）分别分析奇点类型，证明均为Type I；随后对rescaling和dilating后的流序列研究pointed Cheeger-Gromov收敛极限，将极限流形分解为Kähler-Ricci shrinker（满足circle-bundle ansatz）与平坦因子的乘积。

Result: 在所有"two-bolt"、"nut-bolt"和"two-nut"情形下，奇点均必为Type I型。三种情形下缩放序列的pointed Cheeger-Gromov极限模型统一为(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) × (C^k, flat)（m,k≥0），其中Σ只能是以下三者之一：CP^{m+1}、Tot(L^{⊕(m+1)})，或射影化P(O^{⊕(m_0+1)} ⊕ L^{⊕(m_ℓ+1)})（m_0+m_ℓ=m），L为某些N_i因子乘积上的线丛；度量g_Σ(t)是满足circle-bundle ansatz的Kähler-Ricci shrinker。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了由1-参数叶状结构构造的紧Kähler流形上Kähler-Ricci流在三种端点闭合情形下的奇点类型（均为Type I）及缩放极限模型的具体分类，填补了"nut-bolt"与"two-nut"情形的空白，并与先前"two-bolt"工作共同构成对该类流形的系统完整理解。

Abstract: We study the singularity type and models of the Kähler--Ricci flow on compact manifolds constructed from the 1-parameter foliation of a circle-bundle over a product of Kähler--Einstein manifolds $N := N_1 \times \cdots \times N_r$, with metric constructed using the ansatz considered in \cite{DW2011}, \cite{WW} et. al.
  In the earlier work \cite{FT} by the authors, we considered the ``two-bolt'' case where both ends of the foliation close with the ``bolt'' $N$. In this article, we continue our work on the more subtle ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' cases. The former has one end of the interval closes with a nut-type collapse (i.e. $N' := N_2 \times \cdots \times N_r$) and the other with a bolt (i.e. $N$). The compactification $\widehat{M}$ is then a $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$-bundle over $N'$. The ``two-nut'' case is one that both ends close with nut-type collapses, necessarily two of the $N_i$'s must be $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0}$ and $\mathbb{CP}^{m_\ell}$, and the compactification $\widehat{M}$ is a $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0+m_\ell+1}$-bundle over $\prod_{k\geq 3}N_k$. We proved that in all ``two-bolt'', ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' caess the singularity must be of Type I.
  Furthermore, we study the pointed Cheeger-Gromov limit of the rescaled and dilated sequence of the flow in all of three cases, and prove that the limit model must be $(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) \times (\mathbb{C}^{k}, \textrm{flat})$ with $m, k \geq 0$, where $Σ$ is one of the following: $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$, $\textrm{Tot}(\mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m+1)})$, or a projectivization $\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\oplus(m_0+1)} \oplus \mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m_\ell+1)}\big)$ with $m_0 + m_\ell = m$, and $\mathcal{L}$ is a line bundle over the product of \emph{some} of the $N_1, \cdots, N_r$ factors. The metric $g_Σ(t)$ is a Kähler-Ricci shrinker satisfying the circle-bundle ansatz.

</details>


### [7] [Catenoid-sharp index-topology estimates for minimal hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03424)
*Junzhang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了嵌入双侧极小超曲面在R^N中的指标下界可以由第一Betti数和端点数来确定，该估计对高维悬链面是尖锐的。同时得到了Spin(7)版本的类比结论：R^8中指标为1且全曲率有限的完备双侧极小浸入M^7必为高维悬链面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Chodosh--Gianocca近期工作的思想，将指标与拓扑量（Betti数和端点数）联系起来，建立极小超曲面的指标下界估计，并推广Spin(7)情形的刚性分类结果。

Method: 利用Chodosh--Gianocca的方法论，通过分析Jacobi算子的负特征值个数（即指标）与超曲面的拓扑不变量之间的关系来证明指标下界；同时针对Spin(7)结构的特殊几何条件，证明指标一极小浸入的刚性分类。

Result: 得到指标下界估计：指标≥某个由第一Betti数和端点数决定的函数，该估计对高维悬链面达到最优；并证明Spin(7)版本的分类定理：指标1的全曲率有限的完备双侧极小浸入M^7→R^8只能是高维悬链面。

Conclusion: 本文推广了Chodosh--Gianocca的指标估计到一般余维的嵌入双侧极小超曲面，并给出了Spin(7)情形下指标一极小浸入的刚性陈述。

Abstract: We show that for an embedded two-sided minimal hypersurface in $\mathbb{R}^N$, there is a lower bound for the index in terms of the first Betti number and the number of ends, using ideas from the recent work of Chodosh--Gianocca. This estimate is sharp for the higher-dimensional catenoid. We also obtain a $\operatorname{Spin}(7)$ analogue of Theorem 10.1 of Chodosh--Gianocca: a complete two-sided minimal immersion $M^7\to\mathbb{R}^8$ of index one and finite total curvature is a higher-dimensional catenoid.

</details>


### [8] [New Homogeneous Einstein Metrics from Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03593)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用不变量爱因斯坦方程的检测方法，在若干紧致齐性空间族上构造并区分非对称齐性爱因斯坦度量。作者在两因子辛族中证明了一个尖锐阈值，在SU(n)³/ΔSO(n)上构造了两个非对称爱因斯坦度量，对SU(2n)³/ΔSp(n)获得尖锐阈值，并在三个例外三因子族上构造了非对称度量。四因子情形展示了新现象：在M₉=SU(18)⁴/ΔSp(9)上，3+1和2+2两种对称类型均被实现，固定族中恰好包含三个非对称爱因斯坦度量（两个3+1型，一个2+2型），它们两两非同构、黎曼不可约且非自然约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立一种既能保留几何上有意义的对称破缺参数、又能消除剩余爱因斯坦方程的方法，以系统性地构造和区分紧致齐性空间上的非对称齐性爱因斯坦度量。此前此类度量在不同对称类型下的存在性与唯一性问题尚缺乏统一处理框架。

Method: 提出一种不变量爱因斯坦方程检测方法：保留具有几何意义的对称破缺（检测）参数，消去其余爱因斯坦方程，随后从所得检测器中重构正度量。该方法被应用于包括两因子辛族、三因子酉族/辛族、例外三因子族以及四因子族在内的多类齐性空间。

Result: (1) 在二因子辛族证明尖锐阈值；(2) 在每个可容许空间SU(n)³/ΔSO(n)上构造两个非对称爱因斯坦度量；(3) 对SU(2n)³/ΔSp(n)获得尖锐阈值；(4) 在三个例外三因子族上构造非对称度量；(5) 在M₉=SU(18)⁴/ΔSp(9)上，3+1与2+2两种对称类型均被实现，固定族中恰好含三个非对称爱因斯坦度量，两两非等距、黎曼不可约、非自然约化，且2+2型度量在整个正2+2固定族中（除块交换外）全局唯一。

Conclusion: 该检测方法能够有效揭示不同对称类型下非对称齐性爱因斯坦度量的存在性与（在齐性/块置换意义下的）唯一性结构。四因子族表明，不同对称划分（3+1与2+2）可在同一空间上共存，但产生的度量彼此不等距且具有非平凡的不约化与不可约性质，为齐性爱因斯坦度量的分类提供了新的结构洞察。

Abstract: We use a detection method for invariant Einstein equations to construct and distinguish asymmetric homogeneous Einstein metrics on several families of compact homogeneous spaces. The method retains a geometrically meaningful symmetry-breaking parameter, eliminates the remaining Einstein equations, and then reconstructs positive metrics from the resulting detector.
  We prove a sharp threshold in a two-factor symplectic family, construct two asymmetric Einstein metrics on each admissible space $\SU(n)^3/Δ\SO(n)$, obtain a sharp threshold for $\SU(2n)^3/Δ\Sp(n)$, and construct asymmetric metrics on three exceptional three-factor families. The four-factor case exhibits a further phenomenon. On \[
  M_9=\SU(18)^4/Δ\Sp(9) \] the two symmetry types $3+1$ and $2+2$ are both realized: up to homothety and internal block permutations, the corresponding fixed families contain exactly three asymmetric Einstein metrics, two with three equal horizontal scales and one distinct scale (type $3+1$), and one with two equal pairs of horizontal scales (type $2+2$). They are pairwise non-isometric, Riemannian irreducible, and not naturally reductive. The $2+2$ metric is globally unique in the full positive $2+2$ fixed family up to block interchange.

</details>


### [9] [Quadratic Scalar Curvature Decay and Uniform Positivity in Dimensions Four through Seven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03676)
*Zhehui Wang,Jintian Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在维度4到7之间，对于满足特定渐近标量曲率条件的完备非紧黎曼流形，可以构造一个标量曲率至少为1的完备光滑度量，从而验证了Gromov临界衰减猜想在四到七维中的第二部分。阈值(n-1)/n的严格不等式是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Gromov关于正标量曲率的临界衰减率猜想（第二部分）在维度4到7中的成立。该猜想涉及流形在无穷远处的标量曲率衰减速率达到临界阈值时，流形能否携带一致正标量曲率的完备度量。

Method: 针对4≤n≤7的完备连通可定向非紧黎曼流形，假设其具有正的标量曲率，且渐近二次标量曲率系数严格大于(n-1)/n，通过构造适当的度量变换（可能结合共形形变、局部光滑化或手术技巧），得到标量曲率至少为1的完备光滑度量。

Result: 在维度4到7中，若渐近二次标量曲率系数大于(n-1)/n，则流形上存在标量曲率≥1的完备光滑度量。阈值(n-1)/n与严格不等式的条件均为最优，即不能放宽。

Abstract: This note originated with a mini-course taught at KU Leuven by the author in June 2026. We review the definition of weightings (or quasi-homogeneous structures), the weighted normal bundle, and the weighted deformation space. We emphasize the algebraic approach and functorial properties, using sheaves of ideals and the character spectrum for sheaves of algebras. We review the definition of a multiplicative weighting on a Lie groupoid $G\rightrightarrows M$, and give a self-contained proof of the three-way equivalence between (i) multiplicative weightings of $G$ along $M$, (ii) Lie filtrations of the Lie algebroid $A$ of $G$, (iii) dg weightings of the NQ manifold $A[1]$ along $M$. The corresponding weighted deformation space is a weighted variant of the tangent/adiabatic groupoid and is used in the study of hypoelliptic operators.

Abstract: Colding established a sharp gradient estimate for the Green function on manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature, which was extended to positive Ricci curvature by Manea recently. We show that the average of the gradient deficit, namely $\fint (1-|\nabla b|^2)\,dV$, controls the volume deficit in a quantitative manner; here $b$ is the distance-like function defined using the Green function. We also prove a rigidity theorem for closed 4-dimensional Einstein manifolds by finding a new monotonicity formula.

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to the study of Bochner-Kähler manifolds, based on normal form techniques in CR geometry. As observed by Webster, Bochner-Kähler manifolds are intimately related to spherical Sasakian manifolds. Our approach utilises this relationship as well as normal forms for spherical Sasakian manifolds (known as rigid spheres). In this setting potentials of Bochner-Kähler manifolds are nothing but the defining equations of rigid spheres in rigid normal form.
  This leads to a canonical formula for Bochner-Kähler potentials, as well as streamlined proofs of known results on the structure and the symmetries of Bochner-Kähler manifolds. Our approach also provides a host of examples in terms of an implicit formula of the corresponding Kähler potentials.

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for conservative mechanical systems modeled on conic pseudo-Finslerian manifolds, extending classical Riemannian and semi-Riemannian mechanics to anisotropic geometries. In this setting, we define a Jacobi-type pseudo-Finslerian metric and demonstrate that motions of fixed energy correspond, up to reparametrization, to its geodesics. We establish energy and momentum conservation under suitable symmetry assumptions via a generalized Noether's theorem, and provide new analytical criteria for the completeness of both the Jacobi metric and the mechanical system. Furthermore, for mechanical systems with holonomic and non-holonomic constraints, we prove the existence and uniqueness of reaction forces satisfying d'Alembert's principle. These results provide a unified geometric foundation for constrained and unconstrained anisotropic dynamics.

Abstract: In this article, we introduce the concepts of generalized semi-Riemannian submersions and foliations, extending the classical framework to accommodate leaves with varying or degenerate causal characters, such as homogeneous foliations on semi-Riemannian manifolds and codimension-one lightlike foliations on Lorentz manifolds (including pp-waves). Conclusion: 经典活动标架方法中的根式表达式并不与Lie-Tresse定理矛盾，二者在代数结构层面是相容的；该研究为代数伪群作用全局微分不变量的算法化计算提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The classical technique of moving frames allows to effectively compute differential invariants of group actions. Result: 成功证明了$dd^Φ$-算子的Hodge分解定理，获得了Kähler $\partial\bar{\partial}$-引理的Spin(7)类比，定义了Bott-Chern型上同调并建立其与模空间和calibrated几何的关系，引入了Cayley-positive cones概念。同时得到了一系列关于外微分分解和二阶导数/Laplacian恒等式的结果，这些恒等式具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We study the properties of the $dd^Φ$-operator on $8$-dimensional Spin(7)-manifolds with torsion-free Spin(7)-structures $Φ$. These operators were first introduced by Harvey and Lawson (An introduction to potential theory in calibrated geometry. Am.J. Math. 131.4 (2009), arXiv:0710.3920). We prove a Hodge decomposition theorem for the $dd^Φ$-operator and obtain an analogue of the $\partial \bar{\partial}$-lemma in Kähler geometry. Using this, we define Bott--Chern-type cohomologies for Spin(7)-manifolds. We relate the Bott-Chern-type cohomology spaces to the moduli space of torsion-free Spin(7)-structures and calibrated geometry of Spin(7)-manifolds. These relations naturally give rise to the notion of Cayley-positive cones. In the course of proving the results, we state and prove various identities for the exterior derivative and its decompositions into irreducible Spin(7)-representations as well as identities for second order derivatives and Laplacians. The identities we prove are for any Spin(7)-structures and the specialized torsion-free ones are Spin(7)-analogoues of Kähler identities and Bryant--Harvey's identities in the $\mathrm{G}_2$-case (R. Bryant, Some remarks on $\mathrm{G}_2$-structures, Proceedings of the 11th and 12th Gökova geometry-topology conference, arXiv:math/0305124) and are results of independent interest.

</details>

Abstract: We present a finite-dimensional realization theory for bivariate rational functions that are contractive or have nonnegative real part on the bidisc or on the bihalfplane. We show that the realization formula depends only on the underlying domain, while the distinction between the four resulting function classes is captured entirely by explicit matrix inequalities imposed on the realization matrices. These results provide finite-dimensional realizations for rational Schur--Agler and Herglotz--Agler functions, extending the previous infinite-dimensional results. We further characterize symmetric realizations by means of a Hermitian unitary symmetry of the realization data, yielding realization theorems on the symmetrized bihalfplane. Finally, we obtain determinantal representations for symmetric stable polynomials and, consequently, for stable polynomials on the symmetrized bihalfplane. For rational functions over the real field the respective representations can use matrices with real entries.

</details>

Abstract: In this article we consider various properties of a normed lattice, which are similar to semicontinuity (also known as the Fatou property), establish relations between these properties, and discuss stability of these properties under renorming. In particular, we show that a normed lattice $F$ is order continuous iff every renorming of $F$ is semicontinuous. We also prove that the weakly Fatou (we propose the term ``demicontinuous'') normed lattices are precisely the ones isomorphic to regular sublattices of monotonically complete Banach lattices. In order to do so we introduce the concept of the Lorentz completion of a demicontinuous normed lattice, which is somewhat analogous to the universal completion from the vector lattice theory. Furthermore, we unify and simplify the proofs of the characterizations of monotone completeness from \cite{aw} and \cite{taylor} and provide their quantitative versions.
  The semicontinuity-related properties in AM-spaces have some additional features. While the classical Kakutani theorem states that AM-spaces are precisely the closed sublattices of $\Co\left(K\right)$-spaces, we show, using two different methods, that the semicontinuous AM-spaces are precisely the closed regular sublattices of $\Co\left(K\right)$-spaces. Finally, we prove that for a normed space $E$, the AM-space of positively homogeneous weak* continuous functions on $\Ba_{E^{*}}$ is semicontinuous iff it is a regular sublattice of $\Co\left(\Ba_{E^{*}},\mathrm{w}^{*}\right)$ iff $\dim E<\8$.

Abstract: Clouâtre, Ostermann, and Ransford formulated an abstract version of Crouzeix's conjecture involving a bounded unital homomorphism from a uniform algebra into matrices and a unital antilinear map. We prove this conjecture, in fact without requiring the antilinear map to be contractive and for homomorphisms into the bounded operators on a Hilbert space. Conclusion: 本文给出了最大相对投影常数稳定性的显式指标界，结论为关于投影常数理论中的一个开放问题画上了句号，且所引入的技术可能对相关逼近论与几何泛函分析问题具有进一步应用价值。

Abstract: For positive integers $n\ge r$, let $λ(r,n)$ denote the \emph{maximal relative projection constant} of $r$-dimensional subspaces of $\ell_\infty^n$ and $λ(r)$ denote the \emph{maximal absolute projection constant}, respectively. It is known that for any fixed $r$, $λ(r,n)$ is a non-decreasing sequence with limit $λ(r)$ as $n\to \infty$. A natural question is whether $λ(r,n)$ stabilizes at $λ(r)$ for some $n>r$. We prove that for any fixed $r$, \[λ(r,n)=λ(r) \qquad \text{for every}\qquad n\ge 2^{r}\binom{r+1}{2}.\] This answers a question of Basso. The technique used is of independent interest.

</details>

Abstract: We study the Almost-Invariant Subspace Problem for operators defined on non-normable Fréchet spaces and especially on $ω$. We also investigate the case of operators defined on $X \oplus ω$ where $X$ is an infinite-dimensional Fréchet space with a continuous norm and we show that in this case, every operator defined on $X \oplus ω$ possesses a closed almost-invariant half-space.

  \left|a_1+\sum_{n=2}^\infty \frac{a_n}{\sqrt n\log n}\right|
  \lesssim \|f\|_{\mathscr{H}^1}. \] This answers a question raised previously in the literature and it proves that the multiplicative Hilbert matrix has a bounded symbol.

Abstract: Motivated by the weak maximizing property ($\mathrm{WMP}$), we investigate $p$-summability conditions on maximizing sequences of bounded linear operators. Since the naive $p$-summability formulation is independent of $p$ and collapses to norm attainment for all operators, we introduce the weakly $p$-singular maximizing property ($\mathrm{SMP}_p$), based on maximizing sequences with no weakly $p$-summable subsequence. We completely characterize when the pairs $(\ell_p,\ell_q)$ and $(\ell_p,c_0)$ have the $\mathrm{SMP}_r$, revealing sharp contrasts with the $\mathrm{WMP}$. We also characterize the Schur property of order $p$ via the $\mathrm{WMP}$ and the $\mathrm{SMP}_r$. Under relative weak $p$-precompactness and suitable Dunford-Pettis-type assumptions, we characterize universal norm attainment for operators from $X$ to $Y$ in terms of either the $\mathrm{SMP}_q$ for adjoint operators or the weak$^{*}$-to-weak$^{*}$ maximizing property, and derive corresponding duality consequences for the $\mathrm{SMP}_p$. Finally, we introduce $p$-convergent perturbation properties for operators and their adjoints, characterize them for classical sequence spaces, and show that the $p$-convergent perturbation property is strictly weaker than the $\mathrm{SMP}_p$.

Abstract: We determine the growth scale of the optimal constants in the real anisotropic Bohnenblust--Hille inequality. For an exponent vector $\mathbf q^{(m)}$, write $C_{\mathbf q^{(m)}}^{(m)}$ for its optimal constant and $d_m$ for its diameter. These constants are superpolynomial precisely when $m d_m/\log m\to\infty$; throughout this regime, their logarithm has the sharp scale $m d_m$. If also $d_m\to0$, then $\log C_{\mathbf q^{(m)}}^{(m)}/(m d_m)$ lies asymptotically in the interval $\left[\frac{\log 2}{4},\frac{2-\log 2-γ}{4}\right]$, where $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant; this interval has width less than $10^{-2}$. We also solve the extremal problems at fixed diameter and fixed total deficit. The normalized reciprocal-deficit profiles order the canonically arranged optimal constants by majorization, yield exact formulas on a full-dimensional region, and recover the lower coefficient $(\log 2)/4$ throughout a broad class of anisotropic regimes.

TL;DR: 本文研究曲率半平面中带磁场的Robin拉普拉斯算子。已知在没有磁场时，Robin拉普拉斯算子的谱对边界形变不稳定（直线边界谱为纯本质谱，边界扰动会产生低于本质谱的特征值）。本文考虑具有紧支撑磁场的情形，证明了磁性Robin拉普拉斯算子的谱在边界的小而局部形变下是稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robin拉普拉斯算子在非磁性情形下，其谱对边界形变高度不稳定——即便是微小的边界改动也会在本质谱下方产生离散特征值。作者希望探究引入磁场后是否能改变这种不稳定性，即磁场是否能使谱对边界形变具有稳定性。

Method: 本文考虑边界为光滑曲线的曲率半平面，其上定义带紧支撑磁场的磁性Robin拉普拉斯算子。作者通过分析有界磁场对该算子谱性质的影响，研究边界做小且局部形变时谱的稳定性行为，并与无磁场情形的经典结论进行对比。

Result: 证明了在具紧支撑磁场的条件下，磁性Robin拉普拉斯算子的谱在边界的小而局部形变下是稳定的，即不会像无磁场情形那样在本质谱下方产生新的离散特征值。

Conclusion: 磁场的存在改变了Robin拉普拉斯算子对边界形变的谱响应，使得原本不稳定的谱结构在小而局部的边界形变下保持稳定。这揭示了磁场与Robin边界条件相互作用的深刻谱效应。

Abstract: We analyse magnetic Robin Laplacian in curvilinear half-plane with a smooth boundary. It is well known that the spectrum of the Robin Laplacian is unstable with respect to boundary deformations. This means that if the boundary is a straight line then the spectrum of the Robin Laplacian is purely essential. From the other hand, the perturbation of the boundary produces eigenvalues below the essential spectrum. In this paper, the Robin-Laplace operator with a compactly supported magnetic field is considered. We prove that the spectrum of the magnetic Robin Laplacian is stable under small and local deformations of the boundary.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究集合正整数是否可分为两组无单调三元等差子序列的排列，聚焦于典范二进候选集S_A（偶指数块(2^{k-1},2^k]的并集），通过将其可排列性归结为有序加器问题，证明该集合不存在可接受的排列，从而表明典范二进划分不能解决Davis等人的两组划分开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Davis、Entringer、Graham和Simmons证明正整数不能被划分成允许可接受排列的单一集合，但给出了三个可接受排列的集合，并提出了一个问题：是否可以用两个集合完成划分？本研究旨在回答这一开放问题，并验证一个自然候选（典范二进划分）是否可行。

Method: 论文首先建立可接受排列的若干结构性限制，包括轨道障碍、非对称平衡律，以及不可能将足够大的二进块作为连续排列的限制。然后将S_A的可接受性问题归约为(M,2M]上有序加器的可行性问题，该加器需避免单调三元等差序列并满足由15和16值诱导的十五个优先约束。进一步选取其中三个约束构成核心，通过等差数列阶梯上的锯齿传播、传递锁和镜像洪泛归纳法证明当M≡0 mod 8时该核心不一致。由于相关二进尺度均落在此剩余类中，故S_A无可接受排列。辅助使用独立SAT编码和证书检验验证证明。

Result: 证明了典范二进候选集S_A（将正整数按二进块(2^{k-1},2^k]归组，仅取偶指数k≥2的块）不存在可接受排列。特别地，通过约化到有序加器问题并证明其核心约束在M≡0 mod 8时矛盾，结合所有相关二进尺度属于该剩余类的观察，得出最终结论。SAT编码和证书作为独立验证手段提供辅助确认。

Conclusion: 典范二进划分不能解决两组划分问题，该问题仍然开放。本工作展示了将看似复杂的排列组合问题通过结构性限制和计算辅助证明加以攻克的方法论价值。

Abstract: Call a permutation of a set of positive integers admissible if it contains no increasing or decreasing three-term arithmetic progression in position order. Davis, Entringer, Graham, and Simmons proved that the positive integers are not admissibly permutable, partitioned them into three admissibly permutable sets, and asked whether two sets suffice. We study the canonical dyadic candidate S_A, the union of the blocks (2^{k-1}, 2^k] over even k >= 2. We establish several structural restrictions on admissible permutations, including an orbit obstruction, an asymmetric balance law, and the impossibility of placing all sufficiently large dyadic blocks as contiguous runs. For the main result, we reduce admissibility of S_A to feasibility of an order gadget on (M, 2M]: the order must avoid monotone three-term progressions and satisfy fifteen precedence constraints induced by the values 15 and 16. We then prove that a three-constraint core of this gadget is inconsistent for every M congruent to 0 mod 8, using zigzag propagation on arithmetic-progression ladders, a transfer lock, and mirror-flood induction. Since every relevant dyadic scale lies in this residue class, S_A admits no admissible permutation. Thus the canonical dyadic partition does not solve the two-set problem, which remains open. Independent SAT encodings and certificate checks provide auxiliary verification of the human-readable proof.

</details>


### [36] [Alon's Question on Connectivity Graph-Codes: $f(d)=2^d$ for Every $d\geq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02953)
*Chenxiao Tian*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究连通图码（connectivity graph-code）的最大尺寸问题。对于有限图H，连通图码是2^{E(H)}的一个子族，其中任意两个不同成员的对称差是H的连通生成子图。记m(H)为这样的族的极大尺寸，f(d)为使得存在无穷多个互不同构的d-正则图H满足m(H)=q的最大整数q。Alon已证明f(d)≤2^d且对充分大的d等号成立，并询问是否对每个d≥4都对。本文对所有d≥4给出了肯定回答，通过构造无穷多个有限简单d-正则二分图，其上带有维数为d的线性连通图码。构造始于K_{d,d}的一个向量标记版本，对d≥7利用GL_d(2)中不可约共轭类上的概率计数；对d=4,5,6用精确穷举程序验证的显式矩阵覆盖；最后用循环电压提升产生所需的无穷族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon证明了对所有充分大的d有f(d)=2^d，并提出了一个悬而未决的问题：这个等式是否对每个d≥4都成立。本文的动机就是回答这个公开问题，验证连通图码理论中这个上界在更广范围内的紧性。

Method: 对于d≥4，构造无限多个携带dim d线性连通图码的有限简单d-正则二分图。构造分三层：首先从一个带向量标记的K_{d,d}出发；对于d≥7，所需标记通过对GL_d(2)中不可约共轭类上的概率计数获得；对于d=4,5,6，使用短精确穷举程序验证的显式矩阵；最后通过循环电压提升（cyclic voltage lifts）生成无穷多个互不同构的实例。

Result: 对所有d≥4，都构造了无穷多个携带d维线性连通图码的d-正则二分图，从而证明了f(d)=2^d对所有d≥4成立。这正面回答了Alon提出的问题。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Alon提出的公开问题，即对每个d≥4，连通图码的理论上界是可达到的。这一结果推进了对连通图码最大尺寸的理解，并将代数工具（有限域上的一般线性群与线性码）、概率方法以及明确的穷举验证相结合。

Abstract: For a finite graph $H$, a connectivity graph-code is a family $\mathcal C\subseteq 2^{E(H)}$ such that $A\triangle B$ is a connected spanning subgraph of $H$ whenever $A$ and $B$ are distinct members of $\mathcal C$. Let $m(H)$ denote the maximum size of such a family, and let $f(d)$ be the largest integer $q$ for which $m(H)=q$ for infinitely many pairwise nonisomorphic $d$-regular graphs $H$. Restricting codewords to the edges incident with a vertex gives $f(d)\leq 2^d$. Alon proved equality for all sufficiently large $d$ and asked whether it holds for every $d\geq 4$. We answer this question affirmatively. More precisely, for every $d\geq 4$ we construct infinitely many finite simple $d$-regular bipartite graphs carrying a linear connectivity graph-code of dimension $d$. The construction begins with a vector-labelled copy of $K_{d,d}$. For $d\geq 7$, the required labelling follows from a probabilistic count over an irreducible conjugacy class in $\mathrm{GL}_d(2)$; explicit matrices, verified by a short exact exhaustive program, cover $d=4,5,6$. Cyclic voltage lifts then produce the required infinite families.

</details>

Method: 定义crossword grid上的非攻击性车摆放（每个横向和纵向单词恰好相交一次的白格子子集）；对一般网格证明车摆放数量的上界；对稀疏网格（不存在边相邻的黑格对）建立车摆放与带指定-1项的交替符号矩阵之间的双射；对置换网格利用Robinson–Schensted对应刻画恰有一个车摆放的条件。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a non-attacking rook placement on a crossword grid. A crossword grid is a collection of white squares (which comprise across and down words) and black squares (which separate the words), and a complete non-attacking rook placement on such a grid is a subset of white squares which intersects every across and every down word exactly once. We prove an upper bound on the number of rook placements that a general grid can admit. We then study sparse grids in which no two black squares share an edge and show that rook placements on certain sparse grids correspond bijectively to alternating sign matrices with prescribed $-1$ entries. Specializing further to permutation grids, we prove that every permutation grid admits at least one rook placement, and characterize the permutations whose grids admit exactly one placement in terms of the Robinson--Schensted correspondence. Throughout, we pose a variety of conjectures and open questions.

</details>

Abstract: We resolve two open problems regarding uniquely colorable Cayley graphs posed by Klotz and Sander (2017). Abstract: An edge-colored graph is \emph{rainbow} if all of its edges receive distinct colors. Thus, their dependence on $\ell$ is linear for paths and quadratic for stars. We then focus on cycles. Since $\frac{\ell}{\ell-1}<\frac32$, this strictly improves the proposed leading coefficient for every cycle $C_\ell$ with $\ell\ge4$.

Abstract: The cohomology ring of the complement of a complex hyperplane arrangement is given by its Orlik--Solomon algebra. Abstract: We study the operators obtained by inserting copies of a new largest letter into multiset permutations. Let $G_r$ denote the operator which inserts $r$ copies of a new largest letter. Our main result gives a common symmetric-function explanation for the ordinary and major-index operators. For $N\geq 0$, define $Φ_N(F_{N,S})=x^{|S|+1}y^{N-|S|}$. We prove that multiplication by the complete homogeneous symmetric function $h_r$ becomes the ordinary insertion operator: $Φ_{N+r}(h_r f)=G_rΦ_N(f)$, where $f\in\mathrm{QSym}_N$. There is a parallel specialization for the major index. A reverse finite principal specialization sends multiplication by $h_r$ to an operator $Q_r$, which is a polynomial in the $q$-shift $Θ_qf(t)=f(qt)$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Liu、Tang和Zhang证明了Elphick等人提出的猜想：每个n阶连通图满足min{s⁺(G),s⁻(G)}≥n-1。本文旨在对下界进行加强，探索在更强的连通性条件（2-连通）或图类限制（无三角形）下能否得到更优的界。

Method: 作者首先区分正负平方能量的不对称性：对s⁺可证明2-连通非圈图的界n，但s⁻的类比结论因完全图K_n而失败。因此作者转向无三角形图类，证明了min{s⁺,s⁻}>n，并将充分条件放宽为某个最大度顶点不在三角形中。最后利用圈的精确平方能量计算，给出了无三角形2-连通图中满足s⁻≥n的完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph of order $n$, and let $s^+(G)$ and $s^-(G)$ denote the sums of the squares of the positive and negative adjacency eigenvalues of $G$, respectively. Recently, Liu, Tang, and Zhang proved the conjecture of Elphick, Farber, Goldberg, and Wocjan that every connected graph $G$ of order $n$ satisfies $
\min\{s^+(G), s^-(G)\} \ge n-1. $
For positive square energy, we strengthen this result by showing that every $2$-connected graph $G$ of order $n$ which is not a cycle satisfies $s^+(G)\ge n$. The formally analogous assertion for $s^-$ is false: the complete graph $K_n$ satisfies $s^-(K_n)=n-1$. We prove a natural counterpart in the triangle-free class: every triangle-free $2$-connected noncycle $G$ satisfies $
  \min\{s^+(G),s^-(G)\}>n. $
More generally, it is enough that some maximum-degree vertex of $G$ belongs to no triangle. Together with the exact square energies of cycles, this characterizes the triangle-free $2$-connected graphs for which $s^-(G)\ge n$; the only exceptions are the cycles $C_{4k+3}$ with $k\geq1$.

</details>

Abstract: We consider an alternative notion of minimality for C*-dynamical systems, which we refer to as strong minimality. This notion coincides with the usual definition of minimality in the commutative setting, but diverges sharply from it in the noncommutative setting in a way which we argue addresses several fundamental defects in the usual definition. We demonstrate that strong minimality is deeply connected with Rørdam's notion of a C*-irreducible inclusion, giving a new perspective on the study of such inclusions. We provide various characterizations of this property, including for reduced crossed products, tensor products, and subhomogeneous C*-algebras.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合波-热系统在数学物理中具有重要应用，涉及界面耦合的偏微分方程系统。已有研究多限于常数热系数和简单域，而实际物理模型中热系数往往具有空间依赖性。因此，研究在更一般的域（Lipschitz域）和空间相关热系数下的长期行为与衰减速率，是一个自然且有挑战性的推广方向。

Method: 首先利用闭包关系（closure relations）框架，将耦合系统的谱分析约化为单个波方程的谱分析，从而建立与域和热系数无关的强渐近稳定性。其次，借助C₀-半群理论，结合状态空间中的非正交分解方法，将非一致衰减率问题归结为一个残差估计，该估计不依赖于热域和热系数，仅单调依赖于波域和界面几何。

Conclusion: 该研究通过闭包关系框架和半群方法，显著推广了耦合波-热系统的稳定性与衰减率结果，使其适用于Lipschitz域和空间相关热系数，揭示了耦合系统的长期行为主要受波域和界面几何而非热域细节所控制。

Abstract: We study of the long-term behavior of a coupled wave-heat system. The system consists of a wave equation and a heat equation on two adjacent Lipschitz domains coupled by a common interface, with the heat equation being allowed to incorporate spatially dependant coefficients.
  We first establish strong asymptotic stability independent of the domains and coefficients. To this end, we employ the framework of closure relations, which reduces the spectral analysis of the coupled system to that of a wave equation.
  Secondly, we analyze non-uniform decay rates for classical solutions to the coupled system. Using methods from the theory of $C_0$-semigroups and a non-orthogonal decomposition of the state space, we reduce the problem to a residual estimate which is independent of the heat domain and heat coefficients, instead depending monotonically on the wave domain and the interface. With this, we are able to extend the known non-uniform decay rates to spatially dependent heat coefficients, yielding logarithmic decay under no assumptions, as well as polynomial decay in one dimensions or under the Geometric Control Condition.

Abstract: We study open sets of nearly plane symmetric data for the $3D$ compressible Euler equations with dynamic entropy and non-trivial vorticity. In our prior work, we proved that the solutions develop a gradient singularity along a singular boundary, which is a hypersurface that emanates from a co-dimension 2, spacelike submanifold called the crease. In the present paper, we prove that a null hypersurface, called a Cauchy horizon, emanates from the crease, and propagates in a direction transverse to the singular boundary. The solution remains smooth up to the Cauchy horizon, even though it ``feels'' the influence of the gradient singularity at the crease. The union of the Cauchy horizon, the crease, and the singular boundary, make up a connected portion of the boundary of a maximal globally hyperbolic development (MGHD) of the data. Roughly, an MGHD is the ``largest'' way that smooth data can evolve into a classical solution. Known examples show that the only way one can guarantee uniqueness of an MGHD is by proving that certain structural properties hold along its entire boundary. We prove that a local version of the needed properties are satisfied along the Cauchy horizon and singular boundary.

The presence of dynamic entropy and vorticity stretching, both of which are absent in simpler settings such as $2D$ isentropic Euler, introduces significant difficulties that we resolve with novel techniques. Our approach relies on new foliations of spacetime dynamically adapted to the shape of the crease, and a PDE framework based on double-null foliations and the energy identities we derived in earlier work. Collectively, our techniques allow us to handle a challenging new solution regime where the crease lacks strict convexity.

Since these inequalities hold a priori only on a dense class, while the density of $C_{\mathrm{c}}^\infty(\mathbb{R}^n)$ in $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is itself nontrivial, we first prove the isomorphism on the closure of the Schwartz class, which coincides with $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ since the difference of two solutions is an entire $s$-harmonic function, hence a polynomial; the density follows as a by-product. As an application, we obtain existence and uniqueness, with explicit kernels and compatibility conditions, for the fractional Stokes system in $\mathbb{R}^n$.

Motivation: Caffarelli收缩定理在源势和目标势满足逐点Hessian比较时给出维度无关的Brenier映射估计，但此前不清楚在更一般的全局Lipschitz扰动下该结论是否仍然成立。Fathi等人提出了这一猜想，本文旨在回答该问题，并建立更一般的各向异性双边际结果。

Result: 证明了对任意维度d和全局L-Lipschitz函数B，从标准高斯γ_d到e^{-B}γ_d（归一化后）的Brenier映射存在全局Lipschitz代表元，其界仅依赖L。Brenier势属于C^{1,1}(R^d)且满足维度无关的两侧Hessian界。给出了上界常数的精确渐近行为以及一维例子给出的下界。各向异性的双边际结果在仿射扰动情形恢复锐的非交换Caffarelli张量，还获得了高斯扰动的逐点位移界和紧致高斯混合的谱Hessian估计。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了Fathi-Mikulincer-Shenfeld猜想：Caffarelli型维度无关收缩估计在仅要求边际全局Lipschitz扰动的条件下依然成立，无需任何额外假设。更一般的各向异性框架本身也具有独立价值，可用于仿射扰动、高斯扰动及高斯混合等多种场景。

Abstract: We study dimension-free differential estimates for Brenier maps under first-order perturbations of the two marginals. Caffarelli's contraction theorem yields such estimates when the source and target potentials satisfy a pointwise Hessian comparison. We show that the same conclusion remains true under arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of both marginals, without additional assumptions. In particular, this answers the conjecture due to Fathi, Mikulincer, and Shenfeld.
  More precisely, let $d\ge1$, $L\ge0$, and let $B:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be globally $L$-Lipschitz. We prove that the Brenier map from the standard Gaussian measure $γ_d$ to the probability measure proportional to $\mathrm{e}^{-B}γ_d$ has a globally Lipschitz representative whose bound depends only on $L$. The Brenier potential also belongs to $C^{1,1}(\mathbb{R}^d)$ and satisfies dimension-free two-sided Hessian bounds. Let $\mathfrak{C}(0,L)$ denote the upper-bound constant, then \[ \log\mathfrak{C}(0,L)=4L^2+\log L+\mathcal{O}(1) \qquad(L\to\infty), \] and a one-dimensional example with Brenier map $T_L$ satisfies $\log\operatorname{Lip}(T_L)\ge L^2/2$.
  We deduce this Gaussian estimate from an anisotropic two-marginal result. Let $V,W:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be potentials, and let $Q,P$ be positive-definite matrices. Using the distributional curvature bounds $D^2V\preceq Q$ and $D^2W\succeq P$, we establish matrix Hessian bounds for Brenier maps between arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of the reference marginals. Most importantly, the constants keep the directional geometry of the essential gradient ranges. For affine perturbations, our estimates recover the sharp noncommuting Caffarelli tensor. We further obtain pointwise displacement bounds for Gaussian perturbations and spectral Hessian estimates for compact Gaussian mixtures.

</details>


### [88] [Smooth autonomous fast dynamo action on the three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04153)
*Michele Coti Zelati,Massimo Sorella,David Villringer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族非空C^k开的、光滑自洽无散速度场（定义在三维环面T^3上），它们产生快速发电机（fast dynamos），从而解决了Zeldovich和Sakharov提出的Fast Dynamo猜想（记载于Arnold的问题集）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速发电机猜想是磁流体动力学中的核心问题，由Zeldovich和Sakharov提出并收录于Arnold的《数学物理中的问题》中。该猜想询问是否存在光滑、自洽（自治）、无散的速度场驱动快速发电机。此前仅已知非自治或含奇异的例子，本文的目标是构造自治情形下满足该性质的光滑速度场族，并确保其在C^k拓扑下是开的（即对速度场的小扰动鲁棒）。

Method: 本文分两步构造：(1) 首先通过拉伸-折叠-剪切（stretch-fold-shear）机制构造一族光滑、时间周期性的快速发电机，该方法适用于一大类速度场；(2) 然后利用全局Poincaré截面和适当地规定返回时间函数，将周期动力学嵌入到光滑自治流中。在两种情形下，证明过程首先在分布的各向异性Banach空间中分离出理想感应算子的不稳定分布本征模，然后证明该谱不稳定性在奇异摄动εΔ下得以保持。所得估计关于ε→0是一致的，且对速度场的C^k扰动稳定。

Result: 成功地构造了一族非空的C^k开集（对某个k∈N），其中的光滑、自洽、无散速度场均为快速发电机。核心结果包括：时间周期快速发电机的构造及其到自治系统的嵌入；各向异性分布Banach空间中理想感应算子不稳定谱的精确刻画；谱不稳定性在奇异摄动下的均匀持久性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Zeldovich-Sakharov的Fast Dynamo猜想，证明在光滑自治无散速度场中确实存在快速发电机，并且这类速度场构成非空开集，体现了该现象的鲁棒性和普遍性。其技术路线（分布化谱理论+奇异摄动分析）也为相关磁流体动力学和动力学系统问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We construct a nonempty $C^k$-open family, for some $k\in\mathbb{N}$, of smooth, autonomous, divergence-free velocity fields on $\mathbb{T}^3$ that generate fast dynamos. This resolves the Fast Dynamo Conjecture of Zeldovich and Sakharov, as recorded in Arnold's book of problems. We first construct a family of smooth, time-periodic fast dynamos via a stretch-fold-shear mechanism that applies to a large class of velocity fields. We then realize the associated period dynamics within a smooth autonomous flow using a global Poincaré section and a suitably prescribed return-time function. In both settings, the proof first isolates unstable distributional eigenmodes for the ideal induction operator on an anisotropic Banach space of distributions and then shows that this spectral instability persists under the singular perturbation $\varepsilon Δ$. The resulting estimates are uniform as $\varepsilon\to0$ and stable under $C^k$ perturbations of the velocity field, yielding an open set of smooth fast dynamo vector fields.

</details>

### [89] [An upper bound for an exceptional automorphism group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03050)
*Xu Zhuang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Beelen等人提出的一个猜想：对于特定条件下的$\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$-极大函数域$\mathcal{F}_i$（由方程$y^m=x^i(x^2+1)$定义），其几何自同构群的阶恰好为$4(q+1)$，通过建立反向不等式来完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beelen, Montanucci, Niemann和Quoos此前证明了该函数域的几何自同构群包含一个阶为$4(q+1)$的子群，并猜想其阶恰好等于$4(q+1)$。证明这一猜想有助于完全确定这类特殊极大函数域的自同构群结构，从而深化对极大函数域和代数曲线自同构群的理解。

Method: 通过建立阶的上界不等式来证明反向包含关系（即自同构群的阶不超过$4(q+1)$）。结合已知的下界$|Aut(\mathcal{F}_i)|\geq 4(q+1)$，得到等号成立。

Result: 成功证明了该函数域的几何自同构群阶恰好等于$4(q+1)$，从而验证了该猜想。

Conclusion: 该函数域$\mathcal{F}_i$的几何自同构群阶被完全确定，猜想成立。这一结果完善了对这类由特殊方程定义的$\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$-极大函数域自同构群的刻画。

Abstract: Let $q=p^h>7$ be odd, put $m=(q+1)/2$, and suppose that $i=(m-2)/2$ satisfies $\gcd(i,m)=\gcd(i+2,m)=1$. For the $\mathbf{F}_{q^2}$-maximal function field $\mathcal{F}_i=\mathbf{F}_{q^2}(x,y)$ defined by $y^m=x^i(x^2+1)$, Peter Beelen, Maria Montanucci, Jonathan Niemann, and Luciane Quoos showed that the geometric automorphism group contains a subgroup of order $4(q+1)$ and conjectured that its order is exactly $4(q+1)$. We prove this equality by establishing the reverse inequality.

</details>

Abstract: In this article we focus on the solutions of a congruence equation: $\binom{2n}{n}\equiv 2^n \bmod n$. Using the main result of this article and the SageMath software, we improve largely the number of known solutions. Furthermore, we prove that some famous numbers like even perfect numbers and Wieferich primes are connected to solutions of this equation.

Result: 成功证明了排斥三粒子三角Sutherland哈密顿量的Berry-Tabor定理：对于丢番图耦合参数，其（去对称化）谱的对相关函数满足泊松统计，这与Berry-Tabor猜想一致。

Abstract: We prove a Berry-Tabor theorem for the repulsive three-particle trigonometric Sutherland Hamiltonian. For fixed total momentum, its relative spectrum is an affine translate of the positive-definite $A_2$-form. For a Diophantine coupling, the desymmetrized spectrum has Poissonian pair correlation. The proof reduces the problem to a finite-congruence and Weyl-sector version of Marklof's theorem on inhomogeneous quadratic forms.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dong和Shafrir（2026）证明了当ℓM=0时，线性指数映射f的零点集是若干可有效计算的p-正规集的交集，推广了Derksen和Masser（2012）在正特征域上关于S-单位方程解集的结果。该证明可能冗长，作者希望提供更简洁的证明，并探索零集更精细的分解结构。此外，多维线性递推序列零点集的刻画（多维Skolem-Mahler-Lech定理的推广）在这一设定下尚属空白。

Result: 1) 给出了Dong-Shafrir结果的一个更短证明，并额外获得零点集可表为正布尔组合的仿射变换的这一新分解；2) 证明了有限特征环上n维线性递推序列的零点集为可有效计算的p-正规集合（p取遍ℓ的素因子），即多维Skolem-Mahler-Lech定理的推广；3) 由此得到特征为p^a或p^a q^b的环上经典线性递推序列公共值的判定过程。

Conclusion: 本文在方法论上简化了现有证明，同时在理论层面将Skolem-Mahler-Lech型定理推广到多维以及在更一般的环（有限特征）上，并提供了具体的可判定性应用。

Abstract: Let $R$ be a commutative ring and $f(a_1, \ldots, a_n) = \sum_{i=1}^k r_{i1}^{a_1} \cdots r_{in}^{a_n} m_i$ be a linear-exponential map over an $R$-module $M$. Dong and Shafrir (2026) showed that, when $\ell M = 0$ for some $\ell \in \mathbb{N}_{>0}$, the zero set of $f$ is the intersection of effectively computable $p$-normal sets, where $p$ ranges over the prime divisors of $\ell$. This generalizes an earlier theorem of Derksen and Masser (2012) on the solution set of $S$-unit equations over fields of positive characteristic. The purpose of this paper is twofold. First, we give a shorter proof of Dong and Shafrir's result, using the theorem of Derksen-Masser as a blackbox. Our proof also yields a decomposition of the zero set as a positive Boolean combination of affine transformations of zero sets of linear-exponential equations over fields. Second, we prove a multi-dimensional generalization of the Skolem-Mahler-Lech theorem over rings of finite characteristic. Specifically, we show that the zero set of every $n$-dimensional linear recurrence sequence over an $R$-module $M$ satisfying $\ell M = 0$ is the intersection of effectively computable $p$-normal sets (in $\mathbb{N}^n$), where $p$ ranges over the prime divisors of $\ell$. For example, this gives a decision procedure for whether two classical linear recurrence sequences have a common value over a ring of characteristic $p^a$ or $p^a q^b$, where $p$ and $q$ are primes.

Abstract: Motivated by Schoof-Pila-type point-counting algorithms, we study the fields of definition and factorization patterns of Galois orbits of $\ell$-torsion points on the Jacobians of genus-3 hyperelliptic curves over finite fields.
  We show that the degree of the field of definition of the $\ell$-torsion points can be bounded by $O(\ell^4)$, improving the previously expected $O(\ell^6)$ bound (which reflects the size of the $\ell$-torsion subgroup, of order $\ell^6$ for a genus 3 Jacobian). Moreover, we establish a precise correspondence between the rank of the $\ell$-torsion subgroup and the Galois orbits of $\ell$-torsion divisors. Our approach relies on a detailed analysis of the Jordan decomposition of the Frobenius action on $J$ and its nilpotent part.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文针对p-Sylow子群为循环群C_{p^s}的半直积群G=C_{p^s}⋊_χ C_m（χ为单射，R包含本原p^s次单位根），给出k[G]-模V提升为自由R[G]-模的两个充要条件：(i)无额外条件，(ii)要求提升模的C_{p^s}-不变子空间为零。利用这些准则研究k上光滑射影曲线X的微分模H^0(X,Ω_X)的提升问题，并在特殊情形（p为奇数、G/Ker(χ)阶为偶数、X/C_{p^s}≅P^1_k）下证明提升不存在的算术障碍条件。

Result: 得到了两个充要性提升准则，纠正了文献中的错误；证明了在温和假设（例如X→X/G为Harbater–Katz–Gabber覆盖时）下，若χ为单射且G忠实作用在连通光滑射影曲线X上，则微分模H^0(X,Ω_X)存在提升Ṽ满足Ṽ^{C_{p^s}}={0}；给出了在特定条件下曲线作用不能提升到R的障碍定理。

Conclusion: 本文建立了半直积群模表示在混合特征中的精确提升判别准则，修正了以往文献的缺陷，并将结果成功应用于曲线微分模的提升及曲线G-作用提升问题的研究，为Harbater–Katz–Gabber理论提供了新的表示论工具和算术障碍判据。

Abstract: For a prime $p$, a pair $(s,m)\in\mathbb{N}^2$ with $m$ relatively prime to $p$, a homomorphism $χ:C_m\rightarrow\operatorname{Aut}(C_{p^s})$, and an algebraically closed field $k$ of characteristic $p$, we consider the semidirect product $G=C_{p^s}\rtimes_χ C_m$, denote its $p$-Sylow subgroup $C_{p^s}$ by $H$, and consider a $k[G]$-module $V$. Let $R$ be a complete discrete valuation ring of residue field $k$ and mixed characteristic $(0,p)$ that contains a primitive $p^s$-th root of unity. If $χ$ is injective, we present two necessary and sufficient criteria for lifting $V$ to an $R[G]$-module $\widetilde{V}$ which is a free $R$-module: (i) when no extra requirement is made on $\widetilde{V}$ and (ii) when we require $\widetilde{V}^{C_{p^s}}=\{0\}$. The criteria correct several results in the literature and we use them to prove that, if $χ$ is injective and $G$ acts faithfully on a connected smooth projective curve $X$ over $k$, then, under mild hypotheses satisfied if $X\rightarrow X/G$ is a Harbater--Katz--Gabber cover, the $k[G]$-module $H^0(X,Ω_X)$ has a lift $\widetilde{V}$ to $R$ with $\widetilde{V}^{C_{p^s}}=\{0\}$. With $B$ as the field of fractions of $R$, we prove the following obstruction when $p$ is odd, $G/\operatorname{Ker}(χ)$ has even order, and $X/C_{p^s}\cong\mathbb{P}^1_k$: if no such lift $\widetilde{V}$ exists with the $B[H]$-module $\widetilde{V}\otimes_R B$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}$, then the action of $G$ on $X$ does not lift to $R$.

</details>


### [95] [Representation Defects and Cassels Pairings for Congruent Number Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03238)
*Shisong Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Qin的归一化三元二次型表示数缺陷的奇偶性与Cassels配对的Pfaffian一致。针对n=pq且s_2=4的情形，计算了4×4的Cassels矩阵，得到不变量Π(p,q)，使得r_a(pq)≡h(-pq)+16Π(p,q) (mod 32)，并进一步给出了偶类比和高维推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立同余数曲线E_n上两种看似不同数论对象之间的联系：Qin通过三元二次型表示数之差表达的中央L值/BSD猜想相关量，与Zhang对纯2-Selmer群上Cassels配对的显式公式。作者试图用Cassels配对解释Qin的表示数缺陷的奇偶性，并由此获得关于解析秩r_a的更精确的同余式。

Method: 作者首先将Qin的归一化表示缺陷与Cassels配对的Pfaffian建立奇偶性联系，利用Casels配对的非退化性和Pfaffian理论。随后针对具有两个奇素数因子n=pq且s_2=4这一特定情形，显式计算4×4的Cassels配对矩阵，由此构造不变量Π(p,q)，并推导出模32同余式。最后将方法推广到偶数情形及更高维度的设置。

Result: 得到了两个主要结果：(1) Qin归一化表示缺陷的奇偶性恰为Cassels配对的Pfaffian；(2) 对n=pq且s_2=4的情形，证明r_a(pq)≡h(-pq)+16Π(p,q) (mod 32)，其中Π(p,q)为由4×4 Cassels矩阵构造的显式不变量。此外还证明了相应偶类比与高维推广。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Qin的几何数论方法与Zhang关于Cassels配对的显式计算之间的深层联系，将表示数的奇偶性转化为Cassels配对的Pfaffian结构，并通过具体的矩阵计算给出了包含类数h(-pq)的模32同余公式，为同余数问题的BSD猜想研究提供了新的精确工具。

Abstract: Qin expressed central values of the congruent number curve $E_n:y^2=x^3-n^2x$ through differences of ternary quadratic form representation numbers, while Zhang gave explicit formulas for the Cassels pairing on the pure $2$-Selmer group. We prove that the parity of Qin's normalized representation defect is the Pfaffian of the Cassels pairing. For $n=pq$ with $s_2=4$, we compute the $4\times4$ Cassels matrix and obtain an invariant $Π(p,q)$ such that $r_a(pq)\equiv h(-pq)+16Π(p,q)\pmod{32}$. We also prove an even analogue and a higher dimensional extension.

</details>


### [96] [Resolving a conjecture on permutation polynomials over $\mathbb{F}_{2^n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03353)
*Yi Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于有限域上置换多项式的猜想：对满足Tr(δ)=1的δ∈F_{2^n}，函数f(x)=(1/(x^2+x+δ))^{2^k}+x在F_{2^n}上是置换当且仅当k=0或k=1，从而验证了Helleseth和Zinoviev基于数值证据提出的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helleseth和Zinoviev证明了k=0,1时该函数是置换多项式，并通过数值实验推测不存在其他k值使其成为置换。本文的动机是严格证明这一猜测，填补该问题的理论空白，为有限域上的置换多项式分类提供完整答案。

Method: 本文采用数学证明方法，对满足Tr(δ)=1的δ以及0≤k<n的范围进行完整分析。通过构造和分解方程f(x)=f(y)，结合有限域特征方程、迹函数性质和二次型的相关理论，系统论证k≥2时f(x)必然不是置换多项式，从而建立充分必要条件。

Result: 作者严格证明了：对满足Tr_{F_{2^n}/F_2}(δ)=1的δ，当0≤k<n时，函数f(x)=(1/(x^2+x+δ))^{2^k}+x是F_{2^n}上的置换多项式当且仅当k=0或k=1。这完全确认了Helleseth和Zinoviev的数值猜想。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对该类函数置换性质的综合刻画，证明了除k=0,1外不存在其他置换情形，解决了文献中留存的开放问题，为置换多项式理论在该函数族上的研究画上了句号。

Abstract: Let $δ\in\mathbb{F}_{2^n}$ satisfy $\operatorname{Tr}_{\mathbb{F}_{2^n}/\mathbb{F}_2}(δ)=1$. We study the permutation behavior of $$ f(x) =
\left(\frac{1}{x^2+x+δ}\right)^{2^k}+x $$ over $\mathbb{F}_{2^n}$. Helleseth and Zinoviev proved that $f(x)$ is a permutation for $k=0,1$, and remarked that numerical evidence suggests that no other cases occur. In this paper, we confirm their assertion by proving that, for $0\leq k<n$, $f(x)$ is a permutation of $\mathbb{F}_{2^n}$ if and only if $k=0$ or $k=1$.

</details>


### [97] [Odd denominators in the Lonely Runner spectrum for six speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03444)
*Francesco Cordella*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lonely Runner猜想中的Kravitz猜想与Fan-Sun猜想。对于n=6个不同速度，证明了几乎所有满足ML<1/6的元组都有ML=(P-1)/(6P)，其中P≡1或5 (mod 6)，从而解释了Fan和Sun观察到的n=6时只有k=1和k=3的现象。对n=5也给出了类似结果，验证了Kravitz原猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lonely Runner猜想是数论中一个经典未解决问题。Kravitz猜想当ML<1/n时总有q=np+1成立，但Fan和Sun发现n=4时存在反例，并提出q=np+k (1≤k≤n)的更弱猜想。他们观察到n=6的数据中只出现k=1和k=3，但未给出理论解释。本文旨在解释这一现象并推进相关猜想的研究。

Method: 本文首先确定了使ML<1/6成立的六速度元组集中所在的三个无穷二参数族，然后在每个族上精确计算ML的值；结合精确计算和穷举搜索（覆盖速度不超过110的所有2×10^9个六元组）验证理论结果。对于五速度情形，利用Chen关于达到ML=1/5的元组的分类结果，结合相同方法证明几乎所有满足ML<1/5的元组满足Kravitz原猜想。所有计算为精确计算并附有完整代码。

Result: 对于六个速度，几乎所有（即除有限多个之外）满足ML<1/6的元组都有ML=(P-1)/(6P)，其中P为≡1或5 (mod 6)的整数；特别地k只能取1或3且分母q为奇数。穷举搜索速度不超过110的所有约2×10^9个六元组未发现例外。对于五个速度，所有but有限多个满足ML<1/5的元组满足Kravitz原猜想。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上彻底解释了n=6情形下Fan-Sun的数据观察（仅出现k=1和k=3），即由于六速度元组对应的ML值分母q必须为奇数，k=2,4,6均不可能出现。该结果大大推进了对Fan-Sun猜想在n=5和n=6情形的理解，提供了该猜想成立的结构性证据。

Abstract: For distinct positive integers v_1, ..., v_n let ML(v_1, ..., v_n) be the largest number L such that at some time t every t_vi is at distance at least L from the nearest integer; the Lonely Runner Conjecture asserts that ML >= 1/(n+1). Write ML = p/q in lowest terms. Kravitz conjectured that whenever ML < 1/n one has q = np + 1; Fan and Sun found counterexamples for n = 4, conjectured that q = np + k with 1 <= k <= n always holds, and observed that in their data for n = 6 only k = 1 and k = 3 occur. We explain this observation. For six speeds we show that all but finitely many tuples with ML < 1/6 satisfy ML = (P-1)/(6P) for an integer P congruent to 1 or 5 modulo 6; in particular k is 1 or 3 and the denominator q is odd. The proof determines the three infinite two-parameter families of tuples on which such values concentrate, computes ML exactly on each family, and describes exactly where k = 3 occurs. An exhaustive search over the 2 x 10^9 sextuples with speeds at most 110 finds no exception. For five speeds the same method, together with Chen's classification of the tuples attaining ML = 1/5, shows that all but finitely many tuples with ML < 1/5 satisfy Kravitz's original conjecture. The computations are exact and the code is provided.

</details>


### [98] [Prime-power Diophantine tuples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03448)
*Andrej Dujella*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意素数p和正整数r，正约化D(±p^r)-元组（即元素不被p整除的D(p)-或D(-p)-元组）的基数少于2^121，该界与p和r无关。作为推论，正D(±p)-元组至多有2^121个元素，正D(±p^2)-元组少于2^122个元素，且正D(±p^r)-元组的最大基数关于p恒定为O(r)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2005年Dujella和Luca给出了素数p的D(p)-或D(-p)-正整数元组基数的绝对上界，但该结果仅对素数成立。自然的问题是将其推广到素数幂p^r的情形，以建立对更一般的模数的统一界。

Method: 本文将Dujella–Luca的间隙参数论证（gap argument）从素数推广到素数幂。核心创新是用一个显式的环面消元证明（toric elimination certificate），通过初等线性代数验证并适用于所有素数幂模，替代了此前非饱和齐次消元步骤。利用该消元子的显式次数和高度界，建立了统一间隙原理，再结合|n|有界情形的已知一般界得出结论。

Result: 证明了每个正约化D(±p^r)-元组（元素均不被p整除）的基数小于2^121，该上界与p和r无关。由此推出正D(±p)-元组至多有2^121个元素，正D(±p^2)-元组少于2^122个元素，且正D(±p^r)-元组的最大基数关于素数p恒定为O(r)。

Conclusion: 该结果将Dujella–Luca对素数情形的绝对上界推广到素数幂情形，表明素数幂情形下正约化D(±p^r)-元组的基数具有与素数情形数量级相同的绝对界，同时元组的最大基数随指数r线性增长。

Abstract: A positive $D(n)$-$m$-tuple is a set $A=\{a_1,\ldots,a_m\}$ of distinct positive integers such that $a_i a_j+n$ is a square for every $i\ne j$. In 2005, Dujella and Luca obtained an absolute bound for the cardinality of a $D(p)$- or $D(-p)$-tuple of positive integers, uniformly in the prime $p$. We extend the underlying gap argument to prime powers. An explicit toric elimination certificate, proved by elementary linear algebra, replaces the unsaturated homogeneous elimination step and is valid modulo every prime power. Explicit degree and height bounds for this eliminant yield a uniform gap principle. Combined with the general bound for bounded $|n|$, this shows that every positive reduced $D(\pm p^r)$-tuple (i.e. tuple with elements not divisible by $p$) has less than $2^{121}$ elements, independently of $p$ and $r$. Thus, every positive $D(\pm p)$-tuple has at most $2^{121}$ elements, positive $D(\pm p^2)$-tuples have less than $2^{122}$ elements, and the maximal cardinality of a positive $D(\pm p^r)$-tuple is $O(r)$ uniformly in $p$.

</details>


### [99] [The Klein-Vélu septic, 2-Division, and modular function fields of level 14](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03455)
*Masanobu Kaneko,Masato Kuwata*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Klein-Vélu椭圆正规七次曲线的2-除点几何。论文通过将三个非零2-挠点对应的三次方程的根与模函数识别，证明了该三次方程的分裂域正是X(14)的函数域，从而在2-除点几何与从level 7到level 14的模参数化提升之间建立了显式联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解椭圆曲线的扭转点几何与模函数域之间的深层次联系是数论与代数几何的核心问题之一。该论文旨在将Klein-Vélu椭圆正规七次曲线的2-除点几何与模曲线函数域的提升（从X(7)到X(14)）连接起来，为这一经典对象提供显式的算术描述。

Method: 论文利用Klein-Vélu正规七次曲线的三个非零2-挠点，在X(7)的函数域上构造了一个显式的三次方程。然后作者将三次方程的根与使用七次theta函数在τ/2、τ和2τ处取值表达的模函数进行对应识别，并通过分析分裂域来确定其恰好等于X(14)的函数域，同时描述了由此产生的S₃-扩张中的若干中间模函数域。

Result: 论文证明了所构造三次方程的分裂域正是X(14)的函数域，建立了2-除点几何与从level 7提升到level 14之间的显式联系，并给出了S₃-扩张中若干中间模函数域的完整描述。

Conclusion: 通过揭示Klein-Vélu椭圆正规七次曲线的2-除点几何与模函数域提升之间的精确对应关系，该工作为理解高权模曲线的扭转点结构及其函数域算术提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We study the 2-division of the Klein-Vélu elliptic normal septic. Its three non-zero 2-torsion points give rise to an explicit cubic equation over the function field of $X(7)$. We identify the three roots of this cubic with modular functions expressed in terms of septic theta functions at $τ/2,τ$, and $2τ$, and show that its splitting field is precisely the function field of $X(14)$. This gives an explicit link between the 2-division geometry of the Klein-Vélu septic and the passage from full level 7 to full level $14$. Several intermediate modular function fields in the resulting $S_3$-extension are also described.

</details>


### [100] [The derived set of multiple star polylogarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03653)
*Jiangtao Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了单位圆上的多重星形多对数（multiple star polylogarithms）的二维问题。作者证明了每个平移后的多重星形多对数是完全单调序列的生成函数，从而具有归一化的Hausdorff-Stieltjes表示。随后证明了这些函数及其无限深度极限在半平面Re z<1上是单叶的。通过有限指标的密度满足关于逆字典序的严格单调似然比序，结合Hausdorff-Stieltjes类的闭包性质和极限商论证，证明了上半圆上每条平移曲线的辐角严格增加而模长严格减小，下半圆由共轭得到。同一思想给出了不同指标所附曲线的严格分离。论文建立了单位圆上的点与无限指标构成的自然对集与去掉点1的闭半平面Re w≥1/2之间的一一对应，在适当的二元坐标下该对应是同胚。作为应用，一类平移的循环多重zeta星值在所有层级上构成开半平面Re w>1/2的可数稠密子集，其导集及所有高阶导集均为闭半平面Re w≥1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重zeta星值的导集已被证明是半直线[1,+∞)，本文旨在将这一一维问题推广到二维情形。作者希望研究单位圆上多重星形多对数的几何与分析性质，特别是其导集的结构，以建立从单位圆上的点与指标构成的集合到复平面上半平面的映射理论，并由此推导循环多重zeta星值族的导集与高阶导集的性质。

Method: 本文采用以下核心方法：(1) 证明平移后的多重星形多对数是完全单调序列的生成函数，从而建立Hausdorff-Stieltjes表示；(2) 通过密度关于逆字典序的严格单调似然比序，结合Hausdorff-Stieltjes类的闭包性质及极限商论证，证明上半圆上曲线辐角严格增加、模长严格减小的性质；(3) 利用共轭处理下半圆情形，并由此得到曲线的严格分离；(4) 通过二元坐标建立对应关系的同胚性，证明循环多重zeta星值族在各层级上构成稠密子集并计算其导集。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 每个平移后的多重星形多对数及其无限深度极限在Re z<1上半平面内是单叶的；(2) 沿上半圆，每条平移曲线的辐角严格增加且模长严格减小，下半圆由共轭得到，不同指标的曲线严格分离；(3) 单位圆上的点与无限指标构成的集合同去掉点1的闭半平面Re w≥1/2之间存在同胚对应；(4) 平移的循环多重zeta星值在所有层级上构成Re w>1/2的可数稠密子集，且该族的导集及所有高阶导集均为闭半平面Re w≥1/2。

Conclusion: 本文将多重zeta星值的一维导集结果完整推广至二维情形：由平移的单位圆多重星形多对数族及其指标结构所诱导的导集恰为闭半平面Re w≥1/2。这一结论不仅深化了对多重星形多对数几何性质的理解，还揭示了循环多重zeta星值的聚点结构——其导集与所有高阶导集均填满整个闭半平面，呈现出自相似性的极限分布特征。

Abstract: The derived set of multiple zeta-star values is the half-line $[1,+\infty)$. In this paper we study the corresponding two-dimensional problem for multiple star polylogarithms on the unit circle. We first prove that every shifted multiple star polylogarithm is the generating function of a completely monotone sequence and hence admits a normalized Hausdorff--Stieltjes representation. Both the shifted and the unshifted functions, as well as their infinite-depth limits, are shown to be univalent on the half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,z<1$.
  For finite indices, the representing densities satisfy a strict monotone likelihood-ratio order with respect to the reverse lexicographic order. Together with closure properties of the Hausdorff--Stieltjes class and a limiting quotient argument, this shows that, along the upper semicircle, the argument of each translated curve increases strictly while its modulus decreases strictly; the corresponding lower-semicircle statement follows by conjugation. The same idea gives strict separation of the curves attached to different indices. We then establish a one-to-one correspondence between a natural set of pairs consisting of a point of the unit circle and an infinite index, and the closed half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,w\geq \frac12$ with the point $1$ removed. Under a binary coordinate on the index set, this correspondence is a homeomorphism.
  As an application, a subclass of shifted cyclotomic multiple zeta-star values of all levels form a countable dense subset of the open half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,w>\frac12$. Consequently, the derived set, and every higher derived set, of this cyclotomic family is the closed half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,w\geq\frac12$.

</details>


### [101] [The $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-algebra structure of multiple Eisenstein series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03777)
*Henrik Bachmann,Hayato Kanno*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了多重Eisenstein级数的代数是sl₂-代数，按权值分次，并与q-模拟多重zeta值关于权值滤过的associated graded代数（作为分次Q-代数）同构。核心工具是一个估计，将多重Eisenstein级数的通常定义和与依赖于整数N的格序和进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重Eisenstein级数与多重zeta值及其q-模拟之间有深层联系，揭示其代数结构有助于理解这些对象的算术和模性质。作者希望建立多重Eisenstein级数的代数结构（特别是其sl₂结构和权值分次），并将其与q-模拟多重zeta值的代数联系起来。

Method: 文章通过证明多重Eisenstein级数代数具有权值分次结构，满足sl₂-代数的公理；核心技术手段是一个关键的估计，该估计将多重Eisenstein级数通常的定义和（即对标准格序的求和）与按某种依赖于整数N的格序进行的求和在差异上进行控制，从而建立所需的代数同构。

Result: 主要结果：1) 多重Eisenstein级数的代数是sl₂-代数，且在取复线性张成后仍按权值分次；2) 该代数作为分次Q-代数，与多重zeta值的q-模拟关于权值滤过的associated graded代数同构。

Conclusion: 多重Eisenstein级数代数与q-模拟多重zeta值代数通过权值滤过建立了精确的同构关系，揭示了这两个对象之间深层的结构联系，并为后续研究多重Eisenstein级数的代数与算术性质提供了基础框架。

Abstract: We prove that the algebra of multiple Eisenstein series is an $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-algebra. In particular, we show that it is graded by weight, which also holds after taking complex linear spans. Further, we identify it as a graded $\mathbb{Q}$-algebra with the associated graded algebra of $q$-analogues of multiple zeta values with respect to the weight filtration. The main ingredient is an estimate that compares the usual defining sums of multiple Eisenstein series with sums over a lattice order depending on an integer $N$.

</details>


### [102] [Supersingular Elliptic Curves Without Inseparable Small Degree Endomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03839)
*Nicolas Swanson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究超奇异椭圆曲线到其Frobenius共轭的等位映射存在性问题。对于固定特征p，δ(p)表示确保从任意超奇异曲线E到其Frobenius共轭E^(p)存在等位映射所需的最小次数。作者将δ(p)的存在性判定问题转化为三维正定整系数二次型问题，并利用Voronoi三维完美型理论刻画δ(p)接近最大值的情形。对于充分大的素数，δ(p)达到上界当且仅当p可由九个显式三次多项式之一表示，从而将此类素数的无穷性归结为这些三次多项式是否无穷多次取素数值。此外还证明对几乎所有素数，δ(p)至少比其上界低p^(1/6-o(1))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等位映射是超奇异椭圆曲线理论中的核心对象，理解其最小次数δ(p)对计算数论、密码学（尤其是同源密码）等领域有重要意义。然而δ(p)的精确确定极其困难，作者希望通过将其转化为二次型与Voronoi理论中的经典工具，来深入刻画δ(p)在何时取到或接近其理论上界，进而揭示这一问题与素数在特定三次多项式下的表示之间的深刻联系。

Method: 作者首先将关于δ(p)的存在性问题翻译为正定整系数三元二次型上的判定问题，建立等位映射次数与二次型性质之间的对应关系。随后利用Voronoi关于三维空间中完美型（perfect forms）的理论，系统分类并刻画δ(p)接近最大值的情形。对于充分大的素数，通过精细分析这些二次型的最短向量等不变量，推导出δ(p)达到上界的充要条件，即p可表示为九个特定的显式三次多项式之一。最后借助解析数论中关于素数分布的定量估计，证明所求下界p^{1/6-o(1)}在很大程度上普遍成立。

Result: 对于充分大的素数，本文完全刻画了δ(p)达到其理论上界的情形，即当且仅当p可由九个显式三次多项式之一所表示。这一结果将此类素数的无穷性问题精确地归约为一个三次多项式族是否无穷多次取素数值的经典问题。此外，作者证明了对几乎所有素数，δ(p)至少比其上界低p^{1/6-o(1)}的量级，表明δ(p)接近上界是一种稀有的例外现象。

Conclusion: 通过将超奇异等位映射的存在性问题转化为三维二次型理论并结合Voronoi完美型理论，本文给出了δ(p)取到或接近最大值时的精确刻画。这既深化了对超奇异曲线等位映射结构的理解，也将一个看似数论几何的问题与素数表示三次多项式这一经典算术问题建立了富有启发性的联系。

Abstract: For a fixed characteristic $p$, let $δ(p)$ denote the smallest degree needed to guarantee the existence of an isogeny from any supersingular curve $E$ to its Frobenius conjugate $E^{(p)}$. We translate existence questions concerning $δ(p)$ into questions about positive definite integral ternary quadratic forms and use Voronoi's theory of perfect forms in dimension three to characterize when $δ(p)$ is nearly maximal. For sufficiently large primes, we show that $δ(p)$ attains its upper bound precisely when $p$ is represented by one of nine explicit cubic polynomials, reducing the infinitude of such primes to whether one of these cubics takes prime values infinitely often. We also prove that, for almost all primes, $δ(p)$ lies at least $p^{1/6-o(1)}$ below its upper bound.

</details>


### [103] [Overcolored Partition $k$-tuples Restricted by Parity of the Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03926)
*M. P. Thejitha,S. N. Fathima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究计数超色分拆k元组的组合对象$\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$（偶部与奇部分别用r和s种颜色着色），推广了Chacon和Sellers的结果，建立其在素数p模下的可除性性质，并给出模2的幂的新同余式，方法基于theta函数恒等式与模形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Chacon和Sellers关于超色分拆k元组计数函数的若干结果，并揭示这些函数在素数模及2的幂模下的同余性质，进一步丰富分拆同余理论。

Method: 利用theta函数恒等式和模形式理论作为主要技术工具，对不同的r、s和k参数族进行分析，推导模p可除性及模2幂的同余关系。

Result: 建立了$\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$在素数p模下的可除性性质，扩展了Chacon和Sellers针对若干r、s和k族的结果，并获得了模2幂的新同余式。

Conclusion: 通过对theta函数恒等式和模形式的运用，成功推广了已有的超色分拆计数结果，并获得了新的同余性质，为该领域提供了更广泛的结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the combinatorial object $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ which counts the overcolored partition $k$-tuples wherein both even and odd parts are colored with $r$ and $s$ colors, respectively. We extend results of Chacon and Sellers for several families of $r,s$ and $k$. We also establish divisibility properties for $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ modulo prime $p$ and conclude with new congruences modulo powers of $2$. The techniques involved to obtain our results rely on theta function identities and modular forms.

</details>


### [104] [Arithmetic probability measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03939)
*Mayuresh Londhe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究复平面紧致子集上、由特定代数整数之Galois共轭的极限分布所导出的概率测度。这些测度由无穷多个积分不等式（每个非零整系数多项式对应一个）刻画。作者考察由调和测度与平衡测度的特定凸组合构成的一大类测度，证明了对此类测度仅需验证两个条件即可保证全部所需不等式成立。在某些额外的算术约束下，无穷多个不等式的验证可等价化为仅检查两个显式指定的整系数多项式所对应的不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数整数Galois共轭的极限分布涉及深层的算术与位势理论交叉问题。此类测度由无穷多个积分不等式系统刻画，直接验证所有不等式在计算上不可行。因此，设计简化判据（如将无穷约束约化为有限个条件）具有重要的理论与实用价值。

Method: 论文首先确定由调和测度与平衡测度的特定凸组合所构成的研究对象族。在此族框架下构造充分条件（两条即可），利用位势理论中的极值原理与测度比较技术证明不等式系统的可约性。进一步在紧致集满足算术约束的情形下，将无穷不等式系统等价转换为对两个显式整系数多项式对应不等式的检验，从而将问题降到有限维。

Result: 建立了对一大类凸组合测度（调和与平衡测度）满足所有积分不等式的两个充分条件；在额外算术假设下，证明无穷不等式系统等价于仅检验两个显式指定的整系数多项式对应的不等式，显著简化了验证过程。

Conclusion: 该工作为Galois共轭极限分布测度的刻画提供了可操作的判据，将原本无穷维的约束条件系统有效约化为有限检查，推动了代数整数极限测度理论的发展，并为后续计算与应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider probability measures on compact subsets of the complex plane arising as limiting distributions of Galois conjugates of certain algebraic integers. These measures are characterized by infinitely many integral inequalities, one for each nonzero integer polynomial. We study a broad family of measures given by particular convex combinations of harmonic and equilibrium measures. We then show that, for measures in this family, it suffices to verify two conditions to guarantee all the required inequalities. Under additional arithmetic constraints on the underlying compact sets, we further show that the infinite collection of inequalities is equivalent to checking the inequalities associated with two explicitly specified integer polynomials.

</details>


### [105] [The groups $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{13})$ and $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{19})$ are Galois over $\mathbb{Q}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03946)
*Emir Eray Karabiyik*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明SL₂(𝔽₁₃)和SL₂(𝔽₁₉)是ℚ的全实扩张的伽罗瓦群。通过找到次数分别为14和20的多项式，其分裂域的伽罗瓦群为PSL₂(𝔽ₗ)，满足Böge准则并通过中心嵌入问题的适当解得到SL₂(𝔽ₗ)，再利用二次扭曲选择全实结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆伽罗瓦问题的一个经典方向：判定哪些有限群可作为ℚ的伽罗瓦群，特别是对SL₂(𝔽ₗ)这类射影线性群的覆盖群，并进一步要求扩张为全实的，这为反向伽罗瓦问题提供更精细的构造。

Method: 对ℓ=13，通过一个亏格一的Hurwitz族构造次数14的覆盖来寻找相应多项式；对ℓ=19，利用Shimura曲线X₆*(1)的Yang模方程寻找次数20的多项式。两类多项式分裂域的伽罗瓦群均为PSL₂(𝔽ₗ)，满足Böge准则，并通过对中心嵌入问题的适当求解提升到SL₂(𝔽ₗ)，最后通过二次扭曲使扩张全实。

Result: 成功构造了SL₂(𝔽₁₃)和SL₂(𝔽₁₉)作为ℚ的全实伽罗瓦群，分别通过次数14和20的多项式实现，利用不同的几何/模方法（Hurwitz族与Shimura曲线模方程）。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，更一般的SL₂(𝔽ₗ)可以在全实扩张中实现，展示了逆伽罗瓦问题中从PSL₂提升到其中心覆盖的可解性，并体现了Hurwitz理论、Shimura曲线与算术几何在逆伽罗瓦问题中的有效应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{13})$ and $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{19})$ are Galois groups of totally real extensions of $\mathbb{Q}$. For each of these primes $\ell$, we find a polynomial of degree $\ell+1$ whose splitting field has Galois group $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$. These fields satisfy Böge's criterion and the associated central embedding problem has a proper solution with Galois group $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$. One can choose the resulting fields totally real via a quadratic twist. The degree $14$ polynomial is found through a genus one Hurwitz family of degree 14 covers. The degree 20 polynomial is found using Yang's modular equations for the Shimura curve $X_6^*(1)$.

</details>


### [106] [Galois representations ramified at one prime via relative deformation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03954)
*Anwesh Ray*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了无穷多个定义在G(Z_p)上的连续表示ρ:G_{p}→G(Z_p)，这些表示仅在p处分歧，且像包含G^der(Z_p)的主同余子群。对中心维数为1的情况，表示可进一步取为开映像。对G=GL_n，所有根理论条件对每个奇素数自动满足，从而对每个奇p和n>1，获得无穷多个开映像的G_{p}→GL_n(Z_p)表示，去除了先前工作中的弱Vandiver型假设及p≥7的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续作者此前关于GL_n值、仅在单个素数处分歧的表示的工作，旨在将构造推广到一般的分裂连通约化群G，并消除此前构造中对弱Vandiver型假设和素数p≥7的限制。

Method: 采用相对提升（relative lifting）论证取代此前使用的剩余无阻碍性（residual unobstructedness）。假设分裂极大环面存在与每个单根配对为奇数的余特征，并对Lie(G^der) mod p的约化施加显式的根理论条件，在此框架下利用变形环和提升理论构造所需的表示。

Result: 对任意奇素数p及满足条件的约化群G，构造出无穷多个仅在p处分歧的连续表示，其像包含G^der(Z_p)的主同余子群；中心维数为1时表示可为开映像。对GL_n（n>1），所有根理论条件对每个奇素数自动成立，从而去除了弱Vandiver型假设和p≥7的限制。

Conclusion: 通过相对提升论证成功推广了单素数分歧表示构造到一般分裂连通约化群，并完全消除了GL_n情形下此前不必要的数论假设和素数下界限制。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $\mathbf{G}/\mathbb Z$ be a split connected reductive group with $\operatorname{dim} Z(\mathbf{G})\leq1$. Assume that a split maximal torus of $\mathbf{G}$ admits a cocharacter whose pairing with every simple root is odd, and impose an explicit root-theoretic condition on the reduction of $\mathrm{Lie}(\mathbf{G}^{\mathrm{der}})$ modulo $p$. We construct infinitely many continuous representations $ρ:G_{\{p\}}\longrightarrow\mathbf{G}(\mathbb{Z}_p)$ which are unramified at every finite prime different from $p$ and whose images contain a principal congruence subgroup of $\mathbf G^{\mathrm{der}}(\mathbb{Z}_p)$. When the center has dimension one, the representations may be chosen to have open image in $\mathbf{G}(\mathbb{Z}_p)$. The construction continues the author's earlier work on $\mathrm{GL}_n$-valued representations ramified at one prime, but replaces the residual unobstructedness used there by a relative lifting argument. For $\mathbf{G}=\mathrm{GL}_n$ all the required root-theoretic conditions are automatic for every odd prime. We therefore obtain, for every odd $p$ and every $n>1$, infinitely many representations $G_{\{p\}}\to\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{Z}_p)$ with open image. In particular, this removes the weak Vandiver-type hypothesis occurring in the author's previous work, as well as the restriction $p\geq7$ in that construction.

</details>


### [107] [On the exponential sum over squarefree integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03961)
*Nicolas Robles,Alexandru Zaharescu,Dirk Zeindler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于Möbius函数的Möbius平方函数μ²(n)的指数和估计。具体地，对于N≥2，任意实数α，以及满足(a,q)=1且|α-a/q|≤q^{-2}的条件，本文给出了一个依赖于N/q+q并乘以(log 2N)^5的绝对常数界。由此推导出Hardy-Littlewood剖分中次弧（minor arcs）上的相应估计，覆盖Q≤N^{1/2}的整个范围。该结果去除了Schlage-Puchta和Tolev先前结果中的N^ε因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Schlage-Puchta和Tolev的估计虽然在q和Q的依赖关系上相同，但都带有多余的N^ε因子。去除这个因子对于优化解析数论中相关问题的上界具有重要意义——例如在处理奇数和表示等涉及无平方因子数的加性问题时，去掉N^ε可以改进最终结果的精度。

Method: 证明使用了以下关键技术：(1) Heath-Brown的平方筛法（square sieve），但将筛素数限制在区间(P,2P]内，其中P可以小至log N的常数倍；(2) 用有限Fejér大控制函数（finite Fejér majorant）替代截断Fourier级数，以避免截断带来的误差；(3) 在完成特征和（character sums）之后，利用素数限制而非除数函数来估计表示次数的计数问题。

Result: 对于N≥2，若α满足|α-a/q|≤q^{-2}且(a,q)=1，则得到|Σ_{n≤N}μ²(n)e(αn)| ≪ (N/q+q)(log 2N)^5，其中隐含常数是绝对的。由此可推出在Q≤N^{1/2}范围内Hardy-Littlewood剖分次弧上的对应估计。该结果与Schlage-Puchta和Tolev的结果在q和Q的依赖上一致，但消除了N^ε因子。

Conclusion: 本文成功去除了先前相关估计中不必要的N^ε因子，将μ²(n)的指数和上界改进为仅含多项式对数因子的形式。这一改进对于分析无平方因子数的加性问题和素因子相关问题的Hardy-Littlewood方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be the Möbius function and $e(t)=e^{2πit}$. We prove that if $N\ge2$, $α\in\mathbb{R}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, then \[\bigg|\sum_{n\le N}μ^2(n)e(αn)\bigg|\ll\left(\frac Nq+q\right)(\log 2N)^5, \] with an absolute implied constant, and we deduce the corresponding estimate on the minor arcs of the Hardy--Littlewood dissection throughout the range $Q\le N^{1/2}$. The estimates of Schlage-Puchta [SP] and of Tolev [T] have the same dependence on $q$ and $Q$ but carry a factor $N^{\varepsilon}$. The proof uses Heath-Brown's square sieve with sieving primes confined to an interval $(P,2P]$, where $P$ may be as small as a multiple of $\log N$; a finite Fejér majorant in place of a truncated Fourier series; and, after completion of the character sums, a count of representations that exploits the restriction on the primes in place of the divisor function.

</details>


### [108] [Short character sums of inhomogeneous polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04092)
*Rena Chu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对素数 p 下 定义在 n 元、k 次非齐次多项式上的 Dirichlet 特征短和，证明了非平凡界。当 n 较大时，在边长短至 p^{1/(k-1)+ε} 的盒子上也能获得非平凡界，当 k≥6 时突破了传统 Burgess 屏障 p^{1/4+ε}。证明中开发了 Burgess 放大方法的新变体，将问题归结为加法特征和的估计，这是 Burgess 型方法首次在非齐次情形取得成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，Dirichlet 特征的多项式短和估计是重要且困难的问题。此前的 Burgess 方法主要适用于齐次情形，而实际应用中经常遇到非齐次多项式（如包含常数项或不同次数项的多项式）。此外，经典 Burgess 界在短盒情况下受到 p^{1/4+ε} 屏障的限制。因此，需要发展新方法以突破该屏障并处理非齐次情形。

Method: 本文提出了 Burgess 放大方法的一种新变体。核心思想是将原本的非齐次多项式特征和的估计问题，通过放大技巧转化为对加法特征和（additive character sums）的估计，从而回避了直接处理非齐次多项式带来的困难。同时利用了高维（大 n）结构优势来缩短求和盒子的边长，达到 p^{1/(k-1)+ε} 的量级。

Result: 在 n 元 k 次非齐次多项式上，证明了 Dirichlet 特征短和的非平凡界；当维数 n 取大时，求和盒子的边长可缩短至 p^{1/(k-1)+ε}，由此当 k≥6 时突破了 Burgess 屏障 p^{1/4+ε}。这是 Burgess 型方法在非齐次多项式情形下的首次成功应用。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了 Burgess 方法的适用范围至非齐次多项式，并通过高维参数实现了对传统 Burgess 屏障的突破，为特征和估计及相关数论问题提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime. We prove nontrivial bounds on short sums of Dirichlet characters mod $p$ evaluated at a class of polynomials, not necessarily homogeneous, in $n$ variables and of degree $k$. For large $n$, we further achieve nontrivial bounds for sums over boxes with side-lengths as short as $p^{1/(k-1)+\varepsilon}$, which breaks past the Burgess barrier of $p^{1/4+\varepsilon}$ as soon as $k\geq 6$. In the proof, we develop a new variation of the Burgess amplification method that reduces the problem to bounding additive character sums. This is the first case in which a Burgess-type method succeeds in the inhomogeneous setting.

</details>


### [109] [The subconvexity problem for symmetric square $L$-functions in level aspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04155)
*Pratim Mitra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了GL2(Q)上尖点自守表示π的对称平方L函数在水平方面的次凸性问题。对于导子为p²且具有非二次中心特征（导子p）的温和尖点自守表示π，如果对应的局部表示π_p属于特定表示类S，则证明L(1/2, Sym²π)满足次凸界，指数为1/4-1/168。这是GL3(Q)自守表示L函数水平方面次凸界的首个实例。证明基于δ-符号方法、Katz的超几何和理论以及Deligne对Weil猜想的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次凸性问题在L函数理论中具有基础性地位。此前在GL3自守表示L函数的水平方面尚无次凸界结果，作者旨在填补这一空白，针对GL2表示对称平方L函数（属于GL3）在特定位点有局部分支的设定下建立首例水平次凸界。

Method: 作者采用δ-符号方法（delta-symbol method）来分离变量，结合标准解析数论工具进行估值。关键的创新依赖于Katz的超几何和理论来处理某些指数和估计，并利用Deligne对Weil猜想的证明来获得所需的对超几何和的尖锐估计，从而在水平方向上突破凸性界。

Result: 对于满足条件的表示，得到L(1/2, Sym²π) ≪ q(Sym²π)^{1/4 − 1/168 + o(1)}的次凸界，其中隐含常数对π∞的谱参数呈多项式依赖。这是GL3(Q)自守表示L函数水平方面次凸界的首次证明。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在GL3水平方面建立了次凸界，所获指数1/168虽小但具有开创性意义。δ-符号方法与代数几何工具（Katz超几何和Deligne定理）的结合被证明是处理此类问题的有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the subconvexity problem in level aspect for symmetric square $L$-functions for cuspidal automorphic representation of $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q})$ with a prescribed local ramification at prime $p$. To be more precise, let $π$ be a tempered cuspidal automorphic representation of conductor $q(π)=p^2$ with a non-quadratic central character of conductor $p$. We prove that if the corresponding local representation $π_p$ belongs to a suitable class of representations $\mathcal S$, then
  \[
  L\left(\frac{1}{2},\,\mathrm{Sym}^2π\right)\ll_{\varepsilon, π_\infty} q(\mathrm{Sym}^2π)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{168}+o(1)},
  \]
  where implied constant depends polynomially on the spectral parameters of $π_\infty$. This is the first instance of level-aspect subconvex bound for $L$-functions of a $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb Q)$ automorphic representation. Our approach is based on the delta-symbol method. Apart from some standard analytic number theoretic tools, Katz's theory of hypergeometric sums, and Deligne's proof of Weil-conjectures play an important role in the proof.

</details>


### [110] [Catalan's constant is irrational](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04176)
*Zhi-Wei Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入合适的权函数，证明了Catalan常数G是无理数，解决了一个自19世纪以来长期悬而未决的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Catalan常数G（由Catalan于19世纪引入）是否是无理数，是一个长期悬而未决的著名开放问题。证明其无理性能深化我们对该常数本质的理解，也有助于在数论、组合数学等相关领域建立更坚实的基础。

Method: 论文采用"合适的权重"（suitable weights）方法，通过构造特定形式的权重函数来建立对Catalan常数G的有理逼近，进而利用逼近的精度与分母增长之间的关系来证明G的无理性。

Result: 成功证明了Catalan常数G的无理性，结束了这一长期悬而未决的数论问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过巧妙的权重方法首次证明了Catalan常数G的无理性，这不仅是该特定问题的重要突破，也为处理类似无理数证明问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Whether the constant $$G=\sum_{k=0}^\infty\frac{(-1)^k}{(2k+1)^2}=\frac1{1^2}-\frac1{3^2}+\frac1{5^2}-\frac1{7^2}+\cdots$$ introduced by Catalan in the nineteen century is irrational, is a long-standing open problem. In this paper we prove the irrationality of $G$ via using suitable weights.

</details>
