# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Reducibility of symmetry on $q$-Painlevé equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02927)
*Chihiro Sato,Kouichi Takemura,Ohka Yamashita*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了$q$-Painlevé方程与扩展仿射Weyl群对称性之间的关系，引入了与Kajiwara、Noumi和Yamada表示等价的表示，这些等价性蕴含表示的可约性，并与$q$-Painlevé方程平行平移的表达式相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $q$-Painlevé方程已知具有扩展仿射Weyl群的对称性，Kajiwara、Noumi和Yamada通过引入扩展仿射Weyl群的表示给出了这一对称性的统一描述。同时，与扩展仿射Weyl群关联的平行平移描述了Sakai离散Painlevé方程理论中的时间演化。本文旨在进一步揭示这些表示之间的等价关系及其与方程时间演化的联系。

Method: 本文引入与Kajiwara、Noumi和Yamada表示等价的新的表示，通过分析这些等价性来探讨表示的可约性，并将这些等价关系与$q$-Painlevé方程平行平移的表达式建立联系。

Result: 引入了与Kajiwara、Noumi和Yamada表示等价的表示，证明了这些等价性蕴含表示的可约性，并揭示了其与$q$-Painlevé方程平行平移表达式之间的关联。

Conclusion: 扩展仿射Weyl群表示之间的等价性不仅蕴含表示的可约性，而且为理解$q$-Painlevé方程平行平移（即时间演化）的表达式提供了新的视角。

Abstract: It is known that $q$-Painlevé equations admit the symmetry of the extended affine Weyl groups. Kajiwara, Noumi and Yamada developed a unified description of the symmetry by introducing representations of the extended affine Weyl groups. On the other hand, a parallel translation associated with the extended affine Weyl groups describes the time evolution in Sakai's theory of discrete Painlevé equations. In this paper, we introduce the equivalences to the representations of Kajiwara, Noumi and Yamada. The equivalences imply reducibility of the representations, and they are related to the expressions of the parallel translations of the $q$-Painlevé equations.

</details>


### [112] [Certified Spherical MUSIC for 3D Localization under Adversarial Subspace Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03264)
*Albert Fannjiang,Chuong Nguyen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种"oracle子空间扰动"模型下的三维定位问题：给定s维扰动子空间及其与无噪声远场模式子空间的确定性误差界，证明了扰动后的球面MUSIC目标函数在每一个感兴趣的位置附近具有唯一的强凸井，且可用固定步长梯度下降法线性收敛到唯一极小值。通过框架分析给出了云分离条件κδ_X≥C·s^{2/3}的充分条件，并构造了多种反例说明相关界的最优性。最后给出了确定性定位模数的渐近估计R(ε)≈ε/κ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际三维定位问题中，子空间方法（如MUSIC）的性能高度依赖于输入子空间的精度。本文希望在确定性框架下分析子空间扰动对球面MUSIC定位目标函数的影响，建立可验证的、明确的充分条件以确保目标函数在真实位置附近具有唯一且强凸的局部极小，从而保证基于梯度下降的定位算法能可靠收敛。

Method: 本文采用oracle子空间扰动模型，假设给定s维近似子空间及确定性sine-theta误差界。将目标函数转换为1-‖P_Ũ φ_z‖²形式，借助任意云（arbitrary-cloud）的框架分析、绝对相干行和以及Gershgorin定理推导充分条件。方法包括：(1) 证明每个球B_{γ/κ}(x_j)内目标函数有唯一强凸井及值域间隙；(2) 利用固定步长（h≈κ^{-2}）梯度下降证明对井的不变形并给出线性收敛；(3) 通过O(κ^{-1})网格加阈值筛选、梯度下降及去重恢复所有极小值；(4) 构造各种尺度的反例来检验条件的可行性。

Result: 证明了在特定分离性与条件数假设下，扰动后的球面MUSIC目标在每个感兴趣球内具有唯一的强凸井，且并外的目标值存在一致间隙。梯度下降以步长h≈κ^{-2}保持每个井不变并线性收敛。框架分析给出充分条件κδ_X≳s^{2/3}。构造了上/下框架在s^{1/6}和s^{1/3}尺度下的反例，并证明2/3指数对绝对行和论证方法是最优的。对容许单点位移路径的参数类，证明了确定性定位模数满足R(ε)≈ε/κ。

Conclusion: 本文为带子空间扰动的球面MUSIC定位方法提供了严格的确定性分析框架，给出了保证恢复所有相关极小值的充分条件及相关量级估计。指出的s^{2/3}界是证明方法层面的最优结果而非谱必要性限制，为后续改进证明技术留下了空间。核心算法流程（网格阈值+梯度下降+去重）具备可靠的理论保证。

Abstract: We study an oracle subspace-perturbation model in which the 3D localization procedure is given an \(s\)-dimensional subspace \(\widetilde{\mathcal U}\) and a deterministic error bound $\eps_{\rm sub}$ measuring the sine-theta distance between $\widetilde{\mathcal U}$ and an $s$-dimensional subspace $\cU$ of far-field patterns with wavenumber $κ$.
  Under explicit arbitrary-cloud separation and conditioning hypotheses, we prove that the perturbed spherical MUSIC objective \[
  \widetilde q(\bz)
  =
  1-\|P_{\widetilde{\mathcal U}}\varphi_\bz\|_2^2 \] has a unique strongly convex well in each ball \(B_{γ/κ}(x_j)\) and the objective has a uniform value gap outside the union of the certified wells. A fixed-step gradient map with \(h\asympκ^{-2}\) leaves every certified well invariant and converges linearly to its unique minimizer. Consequently, thresholding on an \(O(κ^{-1})\)-mesh, followed by gradient descent from all accepted grid points and duplicate removal, recovers all relevant minima.
  The arbitrary-cloud frame analysis gives the sufficient condition \[
  κδ_X\gtrsim s^{2/3} \] through an absolute coherence row sum and Gershgorin's theorem. We also construct lower-frame counterexamples below the \(s^{1/6}\) scale, upper-frame counterexamples below the \(s^{1/3}\) scale, and examples showing that the exponent \(2/3\) is optimal for the absolute-row-sum argument. The latter is a sharpness result for the proof method and is not a spectral necessity claim. Finally, for parameter classes containing a uniformly admissible one-point displacement path, we prove that the deterministic oracle localization modulus is \[
  \mathfrak R(\eps)
  \asymp
  \frac{\eps}κ. \]

</details>


### [113] [A thickness boundary and modular obstructions for two-set radial projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03400)
*Yuuki Miwa*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究缺失数字Cantor集的径向投影性质。证明了当c_a+c_b≥1时，K_{a,m}×K_{b,n}从任意观察者的径向投影均有非空内部；当基乘性独立且c_a+c_b<1时，存在显式无界开观察者集合使得径向像紧且无处稠密。因此c_a+c_b=1是乘性独立初始块族中全观察者内部性质的确切阈值，并为Yu的两个猜想提供了显式二集反例。文章还建立了仿射平移的模相位障碍充分条件，并得到具有正Lebesgue测度且无处稠密的连续块除法集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究缺失数字Cantor集中径向投影非空内部的精确条件，检验Yu猜想在乘积结构中的正确性，并探讨除法集的结构与稠密性。

Method: 结合几何测度论工具，包括加性组合的模相位障碍分析、Banaji-Yu的固定钉正测度定理、Fourier l^1-维数估计以及Yu乘积定理中的入射论证。

Result: 确定了c_a+c_b≥1为全观察者内部性质的确切阈值（在乘性独立稀疏基族内）；给出Yu猜想的显式二集反例；构造了紧致、完备、正Lebesgue测度且无处稠密的连续块除法集；两自乘积在足够大的r、s下包含区间而交叉除法集仍无处稠密。

Conclusion: 缺失数字Cantor集的径向投影内部性质由维数参数c_a+c_b控制，阈值恰好为1。这揭示了Cantor乘积集径向几何的临界行为，并为相关猜想提供了精确反驳。

Abstract: Let $K_{a,m}$ and $K_{b,n}$ be missing-digit Cantor sets with initial consecutive digit sets, and write $c_a=m/(a-1)$ and $c_b=n/(b-1)$. We prove that if $c_a+c_b\geq 1$, then the radial projection of $K_{a,m}\times K_{b,n}$ from every observer has nonempty interior; no multiplicative-independence assumption is needed for this implication. Conversely, when the bases are multiplicatively independent and $c_a+c_b<1$, we exhibit explicit unbounded open sets of observers for which the radial image is compact and nowhere dense. Rational observers with the same property are dense in each exterior corner region and occur arbitrarily close to the four corners of the product. Thus $c_a+c_b=1$ is the exact threshold for the all-observers interior property within the multiplicatively independent initial-block family. In particular, this supplies an explicit two-set counterexample to the nonempty-interior conclusions of two conjectures of Yu. We also establish a sufficient modular phase obstruction for affine translates of such sets. Combining it with a fixed-pin positive-measure theorem of Banaji and Yu yields consecutive-block division sets that are compact, perfect, of positive Lebesgue measure, and nowhere dense, with both factor dimensions tending to one. For the same family, Fourier $l^1$-dimension estimates and the incidence argument used in Yu's product theorem imply that the two self-products contain intervals for all sufficiently large $r$ and $s$, while the cross-division set remains nowhere dense.

</details>


### [114] [Sparse bounds for maximal rough singular integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03751)
*Yuhao Wu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了粗糙齐次奇异积分算子的极大截断 $T_\Omega^\ast$ 的定量非对称稀疏界。当角核有界时，对所有 $1<p<\infty$ 获得稀疏估计；当角核无界（属于 $L^{q,1}\log L$ 空间）时，对 $q'\leq p<\infty$ 获得类似估计，且稀疏形式的第一元是真正的 $L^1$ 平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知的对称和 Orlicz 凸起稀疏界只能给出平均结构较弱的估计，本文旨在改进极大粗糙截断的稀疏界平均结构，并将极大稀疏理论推广到无界角核情形。

Method: 结合局部化稀疏支配原理、极大截断的物理空间线性化、Rademacher-Menshov 不等式以及粗核的微局部分解。

Result: 对 $Ω\in L^\infty$，获得带有 $(p')^4\|Ω\|_{L^\infty}$ 常数的稀疏界；对 $Ω\in L^{q,1}\log L$，获得带有 $C_{d,q}(p')^4\|Ω\|_{L^{q,1}\log L}$ 常数的对应结果，且稀疏形式第一元为真正 $L^1$ 平均。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了已知稀疏界的平均结构（非对称），扩展了极大稀疏理论至无界角核情形。

Abstract: Let $Ω\in L^1(S^{d-1})$ have vanishing average, and let $T_Ω^\ast$ be the maximal truncation of the associated rough homogeneous singular integral. We prove quantitative asymmetric sparse bounds for $T_Ω^\ast$. If $Ω\in L^\infty(S^{d-1})$, then, for every $1<p<\infty$, \[
  \|T_Ω^\ast\|_{(1,p)\text{-}\mathrm{sparse}}
  \lesssim_d
  (p')^4\|Ω\|_{L^\infty(S^{d-1})}. \] For unbounded angular kernels, if $1<q<\infty$ and $Ω\in L^{q,1}\log L(S^{d-1})$, then the same estimate holds for $q'\leq p<\infty$, with the right-hand side replaced by \[
  C_{d,q}(p')^4
  \|Ω\|_{L^{q,1}\log L(S^{d-1})}. \] Thus the first entry of the sparse form is a genuine $L^1$ average. This improves the averaging structure of the previously known symmetric and Orlicz-bumped sparse bounds for maximal rough truncations and extends the maximal sparse theory to unbounded angular kernels. The proof combines a localized sparse domination principle with a physical-space linearization of the maximal truncation, the Rademacher-Menshov inequality, and a microlocal decomposition of the rough kernel.

</details>


### [115] [Hausdorff--Choquet Angular Spaces and Weak-Type $(1,1)$ Bounds for Rough Maximal Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03785)
*Yanping Chen,Zhengyang Ji*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Stein提出的关于极大算子M_Ω的弱(1,1)型猜想，即在核函数Ω仅属于L¹(S^{n-1})时，极大算子是否具有弱(1,1)型有界性。作者部分解决了该猜想：证明了当Ω属于Hausdorff-Choquet角空间X(S^{n-1})时，M_Ω满足弱(1,1)型界，这是对Christ和Rubio de Francia工作的显著改进。此外，对于一般的Hausdorff-Choquet尺度HC_α，作者证明了α=(n-1)/2是均匀弱(1,1)型估计的sharp指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stein提出的关于带有粗糙核的极大算子M_Ω的弱(1,1)型有界性猜想是一个长期未解决的重要开放问题。该问题的核心在于：当核函数Ω仅满足最低正则性条件（即仅属于L¹）时，极大算子是否仍保持弱(1,1)型有界性。此前Christ和Rubio de Francia的工作虽然有所进展，但仍有很大改进空间。解决这一猜想对于调和分析和奇异积分算子理论具有重要意义。

Method: 作者引入与Hausdorff-Choquet角空间相关的函数空间X(S^{n-1})，该空间严格包含Llog^+L(S^{n-1})且严格包含于L¹(S^{n-1})。通过证明在这个更一般的空间假设下极大算子M_Ω满足弱(1,1)型有界性，作者扩展了核函数的可允许范围。进一步地，作者对一般的Hausdorff-Choquet尺度HC_α进行系统分析，确定其中保证均匀弱(1,1)型估计成立的临界参数。

Result: 作者部分解决了Stein的猜想：证明了当Ω属于Hausdorff-Choquet角空间X(S^{n-1})时，极大算子M_Ω满足弱(1,1)型上界，这比Christ和Rubio de Francia得到的Llog^+L条件下的结果有本质改进。此外，作者证明了在Hausdorff-Choquet尺度HC_α下，α=(n-1)/2是均匀弱(1,1)型估计成立与否的尖锐临界指数。

Conclusion: 该论文是对Stein关于粗糙核极大算子弱(1,1)型猜想的重要部分解答。虽然未能完全证明猜想在L¹假设下成立，但作者通过引入新的函数空间X(S^{n-1})显著扩展了已知结果的范围，并精确刻画了Hausdorff-Choquet尺度下的临界指数，为最终解决Stein猜想提供了重要的理论工具和方向。

Abstract: In the present paper, we consider the maximal operator
  \[
  \mathcal M_Ωf(x)
  :=
  \sup_{r>0}\frac{1}{r^n}
  \int_{|y|<r}
  |f(x-y)|
  \left|
  Ω\!\left(\frac{y}{|y|}\right)
  \right|\,dy.
  \]
  A longstanding open conjecture raised by E.~M.~Stein asks whether the maximal operator $\mathcal M_Ω$ is of weak type $(1,1)$ when $Ω$ is merely in $L^1(\mathbb S^{n-1})$. We partially settle this problem by proving weak type $(1,1)$ bounds of $\mathcal M_Ω$ with kernel $Ω\in\mathcal X(\mathbb S^{n-1})$, yielding a significant improvement over the work of M.~Christ and Rubio de Francia. Here $\mathcal X(\mathbb S^{n-1})$ is the space related to the Hausdorff--Choquet angular space and $$
  L\log^+\!L(\mathbb S^{n-1})
  \subsetneq
  \mathcal X(\mathbb S^{n-1})\subset L^1(\mathbb S^{n-1}).
  $$
  Finally, for the general Hausdorff--Choquet scale $\mathcal H\mathcal C_α$, we show that $α=(n-1)/2$ is the sharp exponent for uniform weak type $(1,1)$ estimates.

</details>


### [116] [Endpoint Estimates for Stein's Purely Quadratic Carleson Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04101)
*Anastasios Fragkos,Ben Krause,Michael Lacey*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了最大二次相位调制的Hilbert变换在小於L¹空间附近的模数估计行为。证明了对于任何满足Φ(t)=o(t log₂t)的Young函数Φ，该算子（包括其lacunary子集版本）都不满足Φ-模数估计，因而不具有弱(1,1)型。正面结果是证明了全算子满足L log L模数估计，lacunary版本满足L(log₂L)²log₄L估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究调制Hilbert变换的L¹空间附近的有界性行为，特别是回答该算子是否具有弱(1,1)型这一经典调和分析问题。由于Hilbert变换本身是弱(1,1)型的，但加入二次相位调制后会改变其有界性结构，需要精确刻画其在L¹附近可积空间的模数估计。

Method: 作者通过构造Young函数满足Φ(t)=o(t log₂t)的反例来证明负向结果，即此类算子不满足对应模数估计，从而排除弱(1,1)型。在正向方面，分别对全算子C₂建立L log L模数估计，对lacunary版本C₂,lac建立更精细的L(log₂L)²log₄L估计，利用对调制Hilbert变换的结构分析和Young函数模数的精细估计技术。

Result: (1) 若Φ(t)=o(t log₂t)，则C₂,lac和C₂均不满足Φ-模数估计，特别地两者都不是弱(1,1)型；(2) C₂满足L log₂L模数估计（记为L log₁L形式）；(3) C₂,lac满足L(log₂L)²log₄L模数估计。

Conclusion: 该算子在L¹附近的有界性被精确刻画：全算子的最优模数位于L log L量级，而lacunary版本略弱，需要额外的对数因子。这一结果揭示了二次相位调制如何破坏了经典Hilbert变换的弱(1,1)性质，并给出了在Orlicz模框架下的精确量化。

Abstract: We study the near $L^1$ behavior of the maximally quadratically modulated Hilbert transform \[ \mathcal{C}_2f(x) := \sup_λ \left|\operatorname{p.v.} \int_{\mathbb{R}}f(x-y)e^{2 πi λy^2} \frac{\mathrm{d} y }{y} \right| \] and its lacunary counterpart obtained by restricting $λ$ to $2^{\mathbb{Z}}.$ We prove that if $Φ$ is a Young function satisfying $ Φ(t)=o\bigl(t\log_2t\bigr)$ then $\mathcal{C}_{2,\mathsf{lac}}$, and therefore $\mathcal{C}_2$ as well, does not satisfy a corresponding $Φ$-modular estimate. In particular, neither the lacunary nor the full quadratic operator is of weak type $(1,1)$. In the positive direction, we establish an $L\log_1 L$ modular estimate for $\mathcal{C}_2$ and a $L (\log_2L)^2 \log_4L$ estimate for $\mathcal{C}_{2,\mathsf{lac}}.$

</details>
